> **Inferred claims may be carried; they may not be cited as evidence.** Not advice.

## 1. Regime call

**Memory is a price-cycle industry in rate-of-change deceleration while its level stays tight.**
`[inferred]` — built on the measured chain below.

The distinction that drives everything: in a commodity-cycle industry the equity tracks the
**second derivative of price**, not the level. Level and rate are currently pointing opposite ways,
which is why "shortage persists" and "stocks struggle" are both true and not contradictory.

## 4. The asymmetry that governs every upcoming print

> **A hyperscaler capex CUT changes the thesis. A capex RAISE only moves its timing.**

A cut breaks both the volume leg and the price leg at once. A raise confirms volume only — it cannot
un-measure M1, because the long-term agreements that cap price increases are signed **by those same
buyers**. Volume up, price-per-unit capped is their explicit strategy.

Encode this before each print, not after.

## 6. Open contradictions carried deliberately (do not resolve by picking a side)

- **C10 — Is "renewables = oil-linked" still true?** Registered 2026-07-28.
  *For the linkage*: four KR renewables names fell **−12.7% to −29.4% in one session** on the crude
  collapse, with multiple outlets naming oil as the sole cause. *Against it*: 유진투자증권's 한병화,
  on record 07-27 — *"there is no reason other than the oil fall; **the idea that renewable demand rises
  with oil is a story from about twenty years ago**… over 80% of new global generation demand is
  renewable"* — calling the move an excessive **"green discount"**; 교보 on AI-datacentre power demand
  turning corporate renewable procurement from an ESG question into a **power-securing** one; KDI
  [7 articles / 7 outlets] on grid and financing rather than subsidies; and 하나·교보 target prices for
  475150 **raised to ₩60,000–66,000** this month (the 07-27 close of 57,100 sits below that).
  **Both readings survive their own tests. Unmeasured. Carried.**

- **C1 — LTA price floors.** They cap the upside (TrendForce's stated reason for deceleration) *and*
  floor the downside (Micron management's stated reason margins hold). Both cannot be dismissed;
  the cycle's amplitude in both directions depends on which dominates. **Unmeasured.**
- **C2 — 009150 Samsung Electro-Mechanics.** Flow out, relative strength top-quartile. No thesis fits.
  ✅ **MECHANISM CLOSED 2026-07-24 (M57), the persistence question replaces it.** A DART disclosure —
  AI-server MLCC, **>40% share**, **₩295.12bn**, calendar-2027 term — supplies the thesis that six
  runs could not find, and the "flow out" half of the contradiction was **an OBV artifact**: the
  B-grade actuals show foreign **+50.3만주 buying** (rule D6 applied). ⚠ **What replaces C2 is not
  nothing**: the contract is **2.6% of one year's revenue** and the stock moved **+7.66%** on it, so
  the tape priced the *signal* (share + "multiple customers in discussion"), not the *size*.
  **Open question, dated: does a second disclosure arrive by 2026-08-22?**

  ⚠⚠ **SETTLED 2026-07-27 — and the answer inverts the question (M156).** DART carries **two** 수주
  filings in the 30-day window, not one: **2026-06-30 ₩453.99bn (4.01% of FY25 revenue)** and
  **2026-07-23 ₩295.12bn (2.61%)**, total **₩749.1bn = 6.62%**. **The 06-30 filing — the larger and
  earlier one — appears in ZERO domestic articles over 30 days**, which is why the desk built M57 on
  the July filing and then spent a week asking whether a *second* would arrive. ⇒ **"the contract is
  only 2.6% of one year's revenue" understated the disclosed total by 2.5×**, and the *"tape priced
  the signal, not the size"* reading weakens accordingly.
  **What stays open, and it is sharper than before**: (a) the 06-30 filing's **품목 and term could not
  be parsed**, so whether it is the same AI-server MLCC line is **UNKNOWN — keep the third bucket
  (C3)**; (b) asiae's *"₩1.5조 LTA"* is **exactly 2.0× the DART total ⇒ unsupported as stated**
  (one outlet, 748-char body, the outlet measured at 55.6% boilerplate — D10);
  (c) ⚠ **the actor axis now contradicts itself**: foreign bought (+50.3만주) while **연기금 was the
  #2 net seller of the name in July (−₩313.6bn)** — the "institution" aggregate every DEEP reads is
  netting two opposite actors, which the desk has never decomposed (**new dig D62**).
  **The 2026-08-22 kill line is unchanged**: no further disclosure ∧ foreign 20d flipping to net sell.

- **C9 — KR's own edge axis is missing from the instrument that ranks its sectors.** Measured
  2026-07-24 on two independent legs: `scripts/sector_flow.py:165` calls `flow_tag(p, vel)` and
  **never passes the KIS per-investor actuals or the short balance** (M56), and separately
  **240 names pass OBV-매집 ∧ RS20>0 while only 49 are 🟢 — all 191 blocked by `vol_surge` alone**
  (M55). So the sweep's breadth and 🟢 counts — which ROTATION promotes and demotes sectors on —
  are a **volume test wearing a flow label**, and the desk's only B-grade advantage is not in it.
  *Against acting on this*: it is a **behavioural change to a live shared module and needs human
  approval** (same class as D11), and re-measuring by hand through full-axis `module_flow` already
  works (통신 3/5 vs the sweep's 0/5). *For acting on it*: this is now the **second market** in which
  the same mechanism has been measured (US M25/M38), so it is code structure, not a KR quirk.
  **Carried, not resolved. Handled at the interpretation layer via D6 until a human clears D37.**
- **C3 — Level vs rate.** "Shortage through 2027" (M7) and "price growth decelerating" (M1) are both
  true. Any proposition citing only one is quoting half the state.
- **C4 — The refining node: margin or war premium?** Both readings survive their own tests.
  *Against the echo reading*: the pre-mortem inferred a war-premium echo from RS-concentration
  (VLO earned ~100% of its 60-day excess vs SPY in the last 20 days) — but **the crack itself shows the
  same concentration** (56.92 → 52.12 → **68.23**, i.e. >100% of its 60-day gain in the same 20 days),
  so RS-concentration alone **cannot separate** the two. *For the echo reading*: on 07-17, 07-20 and
  07-21 **the crack fell every session while crude rose +7.5% and all three refiners rose every day**,
  and VLO's daily increment decayed +3.13 → +1.18 → +0.48. Three observations is **indistinguishable**
  on its own. **Carried as a running counter, not resolved.** ⚠ **Recount 2026-07-23 (R12): the counter is
  NOT continuous — 3 confirmed (07-17/20/21), 1 BROKEN (07-22, when all three refiners fell
  with the crack), 1 unconfirmed-intraday (07-23).** And the 07-23 crack collapse decomposes as
  **gasoline −19.7% vs distillate −3.6%**, so the *diesel* bottleneck that the margin thesis
  actually rests on is **intact** — a single 3-2-1 headline hid two opposite moves. ★ New
  evidence on the other side (M34, dig D23 closed): **DAL/UAL/FDX/LUV all disclosed fuel costs
  +66% to +84% YoY, and UAL and LUV cut or missed Q3 guidance explicitly citing fuel/crack
  costs** — real dollars are moving through the crack, so the margin is economically real. That
  **still does not separate margin from war premium**; it removes the "the crack is a paper
  number" objection only.
- **C5 — Security/observability: the strongest measured momentum meets the weakest measured valuation
  discipline.** RS60 +66 to +94 vs SPY with positive RS20 is the desk's only A-grade verified signal
  firing cleanly; fwd multiples of 46–122x are the highest in any current file; **and this repo has
  never measured a valuation factor.** Resolving it by "gate on valuation" would let an unmeasured axis
  veto a measured one; resolving it by "momentum wins" ignores that PLAY23 — the experiment that would
  test exactly this — **has never produced a result** (dig D15). **Unmeasured. Carried.**
- **C6 — The un-narrated branch.** Going into the 07-22 hyperscaler print, `"capex cut"` had **0 mentions
  in 24 hours** across the foreign feed while the market priced a ±7.1% move and cut near-term estimates
  4:1. Absence of narrative is **not** evidence of absence — the desk's own measured case is the KOSPI
  −8% circuit-breaker day, which ranked **nowhere** on term velocity. Logged so that if the branch fires,
  the record shows it was un-narrated beforehand.

  ⚠ **Re-measured 2026-07-23 and C6 STANDS.** Same query form, `--scope foreign`: **`"capex cut"`
  d1 = 0, d7 = 1** — unchanged — *after* Alphabet raised capex (S1 FIRED-A) and the stock fell. **The
  market has now demonstrably priced capex-as-margin-drag while the phrase for that branch appeared
  zero times in 24 hours across the entire foreign feed.** C6 was logged so that if the branch fired,
  the record would show it was un-narrated beforehand. **It fired, and the record shows exactly that.**
  This is the direct empirical justification for **S13**: that branch cannot be anticipated from the
  news axis and must be bracketed on observables.

- **C7 — Health Care: the biggest belief-vs-money gap on the board, and both readings survive.**
  Money says **#1 wflow of 11, best breadth (0.19), most greens (6 of 32)**, all six positive on
  **both RS20 and RS60 vs SPY** with OBV agreeing — against a desk underweight carried for several
  runs. *Against the flow*: **Δ −0.102** (one of only two negative deltas on the board), **revision
  books flat** (+0.5% to +1.4% CY EPS/90d — compare the refiners' +51–71% or GS's +19.5%), only
  **3 of 7 sub-nodes** carry it, and **no health-care thread exists among the 145 alive threads** —
  money without narrative. *For the flow*: risk-off is the obvious alternative (VIX 16.64→19.13,
  crude +5.2%, real 10y at a 120-day high) but **credit does not confirm it** (HY OAS 2.69%, 6bp off
  a 365-day low; NFCI loosening a 5th week). **Six greens on one date is n≈1, not n=6 (S1).**
  **Indistinguishable (C4). Carried, not resolved. Re-check 2026-08-06.**

- **C8 — Information Technology is one label over three legs that want opposite verdicts.**
  Registered as scenario **S13** rather than argued here. The spenders (MSFT; META/GOOGL are GICS
  Comm Services) may deserve *more* underweight on **multiple compression** while the suppliers
  (MU/AMAT/LRCX/KLAC, all **positive RS60 and negative RS20 vs SPY**) deserve *less*, protected by
  the same capex raise that compresses the spenders — with security/observability a third leg that a
  blanket underweight arithmetically shorts. **The desk has already measured this exact shape twice**
  (R7: duration REITs vs digital infrastructure, 18.2pp, replicated on two independent dates; M26/M36:
  primes vs capital goods). **IT is the third instance and the only one still carried as a single
  label.** ⚠ **Unresolved and deliberately so**: the resolving observable is S13's cross-condition on
  **2026-07-29**, and INTC's 07-23 print (**S6**) is its first live test — **which is unscored at the
  time of writing and must be scored at the next HANDOVER or logged EXPIRED.**

## Added by the 2026-08-06 `industry_US` run — §5 retracted ledger (append-only)

> ⚠ **All four were written by THIS run's own earlier stages and killed by its own later ones** — the
> **D48 "verify runs after assert" pattern, at its widest recorded extent on this desk (4 sectors in
> one run).** Written via append, never a whole-file `'w'` rewrite (the D165 pre-commitment, kept).

| # | Claim once made | Killed by | Date |
|---|---|---|---|
| **R49** | **"Health Care is the ONLY sector whose entire green set is volume-lit"** — written by this run's own `SWEEP_READ.md §3` and used as the sole carrier for `SECTOR_ROTATION.md §2`'s promotion of **HLTH N → OW−** | **False AND vacuous, on two independent measurements by DEEP-HLTH.** (i) **`velocity` is populated on exactly universe mcap-ranks 1–51, zero exceptions** (rank 51 RTX populated, rank 52 C `None`) — **HLTH's three greens sit at ranks AMGN 66 / BMY 111 / IDXX 254, so the velocity path never existed for them.** The property was a **coverage boundary**, not a sector characteristic. (ii) **Four** sectors have all-volume-lit green sets (DISC 3, HLTH 3, **INDU 5**, RE 1) ⇒ *"the only"* is simply wrong. ★ **What survives**: the greens are volume-confirmed (AMGN 1.27 · BMY 1.55 · IDXX 1.43). **What is withdrawn**: that this distinguishes Health Care, and the promotion built on it. ⇒ **the OW− is WITHDRAWN and HLTH reverts to N.** ⚠⚠ **And the promotion was made on a bar where the desk's OWN 07-30 registered anti-signal had already fired** — *"XLV's 5-day excess vs SPY turning negative while SPY rises"*, measured **SPY +5.53% / XLV −1.25% / excess −6.78pp** — **which no stage read.** ⚠ Scope (C4): this does **not** assert Health Care should be UW; it restores **N**, the state the desk already had | 2026-08-06 |
| **R50** | **"Financials' rank-1 breadth (0.15, highest of 11) and its 5 new-🟢 carry the promotion to OW"** — written by this run's own `SECTOR_ROTATION.md §2` | **An instrument artifact, and the desk had already printed the counterfactual.** DEEP-FIN diffed four consecutive `SECTOR_FLOW_US.json` files: **`velocity` is populated 51/300 (08-03) → 0/300 (08-04) → 0/300 (08-05) → 51/300 (08-06)**, and **Financials' breadth tracks it 1:1 at 0.106 → 0.085 → 0.043 → 0.149.** ⇒ **the "breadth without velocity" counterfactual is not a model — it is exactly what Financials printed on the 08-05 run.** **ROTATION dropped FIN from continuous on 08-05 (axis off) and re-promoted it today (axis on).** Consequently **JPM, BAC and MA were already 🟢 on 07-31 with the same axis lit — their `new_green` flags fired only because the axis blinked off across both baseline runs.** 🚨 **`sector_flow.py`'s own 3-axis/4-axis guard is run-level (`mode`) while the axis count that changed is per-name, so all four runs stamped `"news"` and the guard passed.** ★ **What survives, and the OW verdict with it**: mega-cap wflow is **rank 1 under every removal** (ex-BRK-B +0.289, breadth 0.130, both still rank 1); the lead is a **40–60 session object** (exc40 +7.59 / exc60 +8.96 vs exc5 −3.20); and **GS's revision book is the best in the sector and un-owned** (CY 30d **19↑/0↓**, NY 20↑/0↓, CY +19.2%/90d) while tagged 🟡 with RS20 −0.3. **What is withdrawn is the cited leg, not the tilt** | 2026-08-06 |
| **R51** | **"Energy's 0.285 wflow−eqflow concentration gap is the widest on the board and is the evidence for the OW− → N+ downgrade"** — written by this run's own `SECTOR_ROTATION.md §2` | **It is an arithmetic identity, not a signal.** **XOM ($571B) + CVX ($346B) are ~55% of the sector's measured cap across only 16 names**, so a positive cap-weighted mean beside a negative equal-weighted one is **structural** — the same shape would appear in any 16-name sector with two names at half its cap. ★ **What survives**: the **downgrade's LEVEL**, on a different and genuinely informative number — **Δ −0.129, last of 11** — and that number is **the refiners' own** (**PSX −0.318 · VLO −0.277 · MPC −0.167 are the sector's three largest negative deltas**, while the only positive delta is the velocity-lit mega-cap). **What is withdrawn is the stated reason.** ⚠ **C1/C5 both bite: the gap was quoted as a superlative without stating that the population is 16 names with a 55% two-name cap share** — **the same failure class R41 retracted as a method** | 2026-08-06 |
| **R52** | **"Materials' eqflow +0.167 (3rd of 11) and Δ +0.204 (3rd) against 0 greens carry the UW− → N− upgrade"** — written by this run's own `SECTOR_ROTATION.md §2` | **Artifact three ways, on a decomposition that reconciles to the reported wflow exactly.** (i) **`eqflow +0.167` is 63.4% two names of twelve** (NUE+STLD; ex-steel it collapses to **+0.0735**). (ii) 🚨 **`Δ +0.204` contains steel at exactly 0.000** — NUE +0.022 and STLD −0.022 cancel; the Δ is **NEM (+0.629, 25.4%), ECL, VMC, APD and CRH bouncing off negative RS60 bases on ONE day (S1)**. ⇒ **ROTATION cited two numbers as one case, and steel — the thing the upgrade was actually about — is in neither.** (iii) **LIN alone contributes −0.1251 of the +0.0713 wflow** ⇒ **ex-LIN, wflow +0.2609 > eqflow +0.2285 = mega-cap-led, the exact opposite of the "breadth" characterisation.** ★ **What survives and is a genuinely NEW finding, not a rescue**: the EAF-steel pricing cycle is real on primary data (metal spread rate **+4.85 → +7.91 $/ton at NUE, +8.73 → +11.17 at STLD**, two independent issuers, two consecutive rate increases ⇒ **B1's kill absent**) — **and it is unrelated to the number that produced the upgrade.** ⚠ **B2 caveat attached: Q2'26 gross margins are NUE ≈ 89th percentile / STLD ≈ 78th of their own histories, so the fwd P/E 14.50 is low because the DENOMINATOR is top-quintile** | 2026-08-06 |

⚠ **Two within-run corrections that are NOT retractions (they never entered the carry), logged so they

## asof chain — prepended entry for the 2026-08-06 `industry_US` run

**asof **2026-08-06** (end of the `industry_US` run — ★★★ **the largest self-correction this desk has recorded: FOUR of five DEEP agents refuted the reasoning of a `SECTOR_ROTATION` delta and ONE verdict was withdrawn outright** ⇒ **R49 · R50 · R51 · R52 retracted, all four written by this run's own earlier stages and killed by its own later ones (the D48 pattern, widest recorded extent)**; 동치다). ★ **그러나 결론은 오히려 강해졌다** — **vol_surge 축을 지우면 🟢 57 → 4 개만 생존하고
화장품·섬유 생존은 0** 이다.
**(iii)** 이 런의 ROTATION 이 **ENRG 를 N→N+ 로, FIN 을 UW−→N− 로 올리려다 둘 다 되돌렸다** —
근거가 매크로 재논증(유가 반등 · 금리 축)이었고 흐름 숫자가 아니었다. **되돌린 사실을 파일에 남겼다.**

⚠ **철회가 아닌 런내 교정 3건**(캐리에 들어간 적이 없어 R 번호를 주지 않는다, 기록만):

**이 런의 철회는 0건이다. 그 이유를 적는다.**
이 런이 만든 오류 3건은 **전부 캐리(STANDING_VIEW)에 들어간 적이 없고**, **전부 같은 스테이지 안에서
스스로 반박됐다** ⇒ **R 번호를 주지 않고 기록만 한다.**

⚠ **철회가 아닌 런내 자기반박 3건 (D48 패턴, KR 13~15번째 인스턴스):**
**(i)** **PREFLIGHT G7 을 쓰면서 `--help` 종료코드만으로 두 도구를 「사용 불가」로 분류**했다 —
바로 다음 명령이 반박했다(`margin_history.py 009150` 이 FY2015~FY2025 **11기 전 계열** 출력,
`module_chart 006360.KS --read` exit 0). **문장을 고치지 않고 게이트 안에 두 줄을 나란히 남겼다.**
**(ii)** **PREFLIGHT G1 의 박탈 문언(「테마 신선도 인용 금지」)이 과잉이었다** — 반증 프로브 5/5 응답
(삼성전자 628 등), 버킷 7/7 비영, `theme-age` n 최대 8,146, 블라인드 분모 d1 2,386 ⇒ **죽은 것은
`sector_flow` 의 종목별 조회 경로이지 FTS 가 아니다**(M545). **PREFLIGHT 본문은 고치지 않고 MACRO §0-a
와 ALPHA §0 에서 좁힘을 근거와 함께 append 했다.**
**(iii)** **DEEP-HLTH 가 「`S52-KR` O2 의 척도가 `D225-KR` 로 창 안에서 깨졌다」는 가설을 세웠고,
같은 스테이지의 통제 실험이 반박했다** — 같은 정착 바를 구코드·신코드 세 파일로 계산해 **31/31/31
동일**(M555). **O2 는 랭크 비교라 단조 변환에 불변이고, 최대 이동 종목(207940)은 정의상 top-2 제외**다.

n 이 11 미만이고 브래킷이 쓴 SE(1.542, n=11 가정)는 과소**다.

## Added by the 2026-08-14 `industry_kr` run — §5 retracted ledger (append-only)

> ⚠ **ID 3-grep at WRITE time (D137)**: 기존 최고 **M632 · D245-KR(KR)/D256(무접미사) · R68 · S60-KR(KR)/S83(US)**.
> 이 런은 **M633–M652 · D246-KR–D251-KR · S61-KR** 을 쓴다. **`-KR` 접미사 사용** — `D211`(dig 카운터 충돌) **사람 대기 7런째**.
> ⚠ **언어 주의**: `handoff/README.md` 는 English-pure 를 규정하지만, KR 데스크가 쓰는 §5 블록은 08-05 이후 한국어 선례가 이어져 있고
> 이 런의 실행 지시도 한국어 산출을 요구한다. **선례를 따르되 규칙 충돌을 여기 명시한다** — 통일 여부는 사람 결정.

**이 런의 철회 = 0건.** §5 를 뷰 형성 **전에** 읽었고(R1~R68, 특히 08-12 R62·R63 과 08-13 US R64~R68),
오늘 채점(`S67` `FIRED-C`)과 오늘 등록(`S61-KR`)은 어느 철회 항목과도 충돌하지 않는다.
**R 번호를 만들지 않는다** — 철회가 없는데 번호를 붙이면 원장이 부풀고, 그것은 이 원장이 막으려는 실패의 거울상이다.

### ⚠ 대신 이 런이 스스로 잡은 것 — **런내 자기수정 3건 (철회 아님, R 번호 없음)**

1. 🚨 **어제 이 데스크가 적은 「08-14 가 `S38`/`S48-KR` 의 첫 채점 기회」가 오늘 실측으로 반증됐다**(**M634**).
   T+2 규칙 자체는 **3회 독립 측정으로 재확인**(08-12·08-13·08-14 전부 「직전 정착세션 −2」)됐고, **틀린 것은 적용 산수**다.
   D일 아침에 보이는 행 = (D의 직전 정착세션) − 2 이므로 **08-12 행은 2026-08-17(월) 아침에 열린다.**
   ⇒ **`PENDING-DATA` 3런 연속.** 앞 문장을 지우지 않고 이 줄을 덧붙였다. **KR 17번째 D48.**
2. 🚨 **미진입 원장 082640(동양생명) 다리 ① 을 「충족」으로 읽었다가 창을 늘려 스스로 반증했다**(**M641**).
   08-13 한 세션만 보면 보험 섹터 β조정 잔차 중앙값 −0.402pp vs 세션 기저율 −1.183pp = **+0.78pp 위 = 충족**이었다.
   5세션(08-07~08-13)으로 늘리자 갭이 **−0.77 / −0.09 / +0.93 / −0.72 / +0.78** 로 **매 세션 부호가 뒤집힌다**
   (횡단면 sd 14.3pp 대비 ~0.05σ). **등록 문언은 「위로 전환」이고 부호 진동은 전환이 아니다** ⇒ `reaffirmed`.
   건설도 같은 검사에서 −0.48 / −0.08 / +0.70 / +1.17 / −0.34 로 같은 성질. **KR 18번째 D48.**
3. 🚨 **DEEP-ENRG 초안이 연간 마진 백분위만 보고 「정유는 마진 바닥」이라 쓰려 했고, `--quarterly` 를 돌린 다음 문단에서 그 문장을 버렸다**(**M636**).
   연간 FY2025: S-Oil **3.0%(백분위 ≈9)** · SK이노 **5.4%(≈14)** = 바닥.
   분기 2026Q1: S-Oil **16.2%(자기 최고 19.0% 의 85% 지점)** · SK이노 **13.2%(최고 15.4% 의 86%)** = 고점권.
   ⇒ **같은 도구의 창 하나가 「싸다/비싸다」를 뒤집는다(C5).** 앞 판단을 지우지 않고 파일에 남겼다. **KR 19번째 D48.**

### ⚠ 철회는 아니지만 캐리의 한 문장을 갱신한다 (2런 연속 같은 문장)

`ic_ledger` L3 문서의 상시 항목 *"아무것도 확정되지 않았다 — `vol_surge` 조차 Bonferroni 미달"* 는
**어제 h=1 에서, 오늘 h=5 에서도 참이 아니게 됐다**(h=1 t **−3.20** n_eff 26 · h=5 t **−2.90** n_eff 4.2, 임계 2.8).
**이 데스크에서 두 지평이 동시에 다중비교를 통과한 축은 `vol_surge` 가 처음이고, 부호는 두 지평 모두 음(−)이다**(**M633**).
같은 문서의 *"부호가 확정되면 게이트를 뒤집거나 빼야 한다"* 가 **두 지평 모두에서 발화 조건에 들어갔다.**
🚨 **그러나 이 런은 게이트를 바꾸지 않았다** — 코드 변경은 사람 승인 항목이고, 아래 유보가 살아 있다:
**21칸 전부의 평균 IC 가 음수**이므로(**M642**) 유의성이 **축의 성질이 아니라 국면의 성질**일 수 있다. **오늘 구분하지 못했다(C3).**

   `chain-hop` then ran normally. **The leg was not unmeasurable; it was unmeasurable for nineteen

   **`INSW` re-resolved `reaffirmed` → `entered`** (10 body hits, and its record-Q2 article is one of
   the 8 body hits for the term `tanker rates`), **`DHT` reaffirmed on the DATA** (`"DHT Holdings"` =
   **0** body hits; bare `DHT` returns three **DRI Healthcare Trust** articles), **`FRO` reaffirmed on
   a QUERY-FORM defect** (`Frontline` = 107 false positives, every disambiguating form = 0).
   **Appended to `HANDOVER.md §10`, not edited into §3.** ⇒ `D258` amended: the defect is *"a condition
   that names a tool inherits the tool's uptime"*, not *"is unresolvable"*. **US 1st D48 of this run.**
2. 🚨 **MACRO §0 re-derived a diagnosis a PRIOR RUN had already superseded.** It called the news
   failures an *"availability window"*; **the pre-existing `D256` (2026-08-13 DRIFT) already says the
   failures are per-query COST** — *"the sweep issues 300 SEQUENTIAL `news_velocity` calls; a
   client-side timeout under server load produces exactly a partial failure like 17.0% coverage."*
   ⚠ **And neither story explains both observations** — a 22:15 `URLError` is a connection failure,

   22:55:00, identical counts of 3,835, the full pre-registered `P55` test). **What is withdrawn**:

   `Treasury`** — on a pool of **487 articles = 0.069× a normal day**. The live server index carried a

| **R74** | **"010950's 2Q26 refining OP fell −48.8% QoQ because the refining MARGIN itself contracted"** — branch (b) of proposition `M-69`, registered by this same run's MACRO stage at ~10:0x KST on the primary-source numbers it had just extracted from DART | **Its own pre-registered anti-signal ④ fired 40 minutes later, in the BET stage of the same run.** The condition was *"if another KR refiner's half-year segment note shows 2Q QoQ positive, retract (b)."* Measured on two primaries: **096770 SK이노베이션 「에너지 및 화학」 segment OP — 1Q26 2,368,674 → 1H26 5,053,621 ⇒ 2Q26 = 2,684,947, QoQ +13.35%** (`rcpNo` 20260515001455 / 20260814002500), against 010950 refining **−48.76%**. ⇒ **A second KR refiner's refining-containing segment GREW in the same quarter.** ★ **What survives**: the observation itself — 010950's YoY is a loss→profit swing of +₩2,069.3bn while its QoQ halved, and the narrative/tape reflect only the YoY half. **What is withdrawn**: the attribution of that QoQ to refining margin. Branch (a) — a one-off inventory-valuation effect — is strengthened by 010950's own **1H26 재고자산평가손실 ₩343.1bn, 102× the prior year**. ⚠ **Scope (C4)**: this does not assert refining margins are strong; it withdraws a named cause. ⚠ **Honest limits recorded rather than used as escape hatches**: SK이노's segment is **wider** than S-Oil's (chemicals and lubricants inside, E&S outside) so the comparison is **not like-for-like** — the pre-registered wording fired anyway and **was not narrowed after the fact**; and **the GS half of the anti-signal was unobservable by construction** (GS's segment note has no refining segment at all — GS칼텍스 is equity-method) → `D272-KR` | 2026-08-17 |
| **R75** | **"S47-KR's scored ratio 재고관련이익 ₩113,700m ÷ 정유 OP ₩532,400m = 21.36% rests on a figure that a filing will confirm"** — the implicit premise of `M-19″`'s anchor, carried since 2026-08-03 and pre-committed to a primary-source check by `S51-KR` | **The filing arrived and the numerator is not in it.** 010950's 2026 반기보고서 (`20260814000827`, filed on day one of the registered window) contains **「재고관련」 zero times across 3,977,008 characters**. The nearest filed concept — 재고자산평가손실(환입) **343,126 백만원** — is a lower-of-cost-or-NRV charge to COGS: **opposite sign, different concept, not a substitute.** ★ **What survives and is the useful half**: the DENOMINATOR reproduces almost exactly — the filing-derived 2Q26 refining OP is **532,378** against the **532,400** carried from a news body, **a 0.0041% difference**. ⇒ **The operative rule is not "news bodies are unreliable" but "ask first whether the number exists in the filing at all"** — measured at 0.0041% error where it does, and undefined where it does not. **What is withdrawn**: any statement that treats the 21.36% ratio as primary-source-backed. **S47-KR's FIRED-B verdict is NOT re-scored** (thresholds are frozen); only the anchor loses its grade. ⚠ **A missing 재고관련이익 line is not concealment** — it is not a required item of a K-IFRS segment note, and that distinction is part of the finding. ⚠ **n=1** (one company, one quarter, one figure) — not generalizable | 2026-08-17 |

| **R78** | **"`TGT` is 🟢가속"** — written by this run's own `EVENT_ALPHA` Card 6 and carried into `SECTOR_ROTATION §2c/§3` as part of the argument for promoting Consumer Staples to a rotating DEEP slot | **This run's own `SECTOR_DEEP_STPL §0`, using the admissible instrument.** The settled 3-axis sweep tags `TGT` **🟡중립** with `vol_surge` **1.01** and `velocity: None`; the 🟢 came from a standalone `module_flow` probe at 22:48 KST that **did** get velocity (**2.38×**). **Same code, same closes, one extra input** (`M717`). ⇒ The 🟢 rested on **the axis PREFLIGHT G1 revoked**. ★ **What survives, and it was enough for the mandate**: `TGT` is STPL's **#1 `flow_score` at +0.503** — a score that uses **no news at all** — with the sector's best rs60. **What is withdrawn**: the tag, and any freshness or ignition claim built on it. ⚠ **This is the D48 pattern inside a single run**, caught at DEEP and corrected in `BET §B` and `ACTION_TICKETS` rather than edited away | 2026-08-17 |

### ⚠ What this run caught in ITSELF — **4 in-run self-corrections (NOT retractions, no R numbers)**

1. **PREFLIGHT labelled the `breadth` contamination "★★ NEW" and it was not new.** The mechanism was
   already `D261` (US, `M701`) and `D263-KR` (KR). **What is genuinely new is narrower**: the effect
   landed on **`breadth`**, the statistic that carried the ENRG promotion — and **the 08-16 rights table
   granted `breadth` as safe** (*"unweighted… they carry more load, not less"*) **while the same run's
   HANDOVER §6 carried `D261`. The desk contradicted itself inside one run and neither half noticed.**
2. **MACRO drafted "the news axis shows the Hormuz story accelerating" and its own instrument refuted
   it** — `theme_age Hormuz` **1.17×**, `ceasefire` **0.81×**, `burst` returned nothing. The corrected
   statement became `P71`/`M713` and is stronger than either draft.
3. **MACRO drafted "crude is repricing the Hormuz shutdown" from the headline *"Crude oil approaches
   $90 again"* and its own price pull refuted it inside the same stage** — Brent was **88.91 on 08-11**
   and **88.52 on 08-14**; the "approach" is a level it never left (`M711`).
4. **DRIFT drafted "no drift — the burst list is clean" and refuted it by looking somewhere else.** The
   burst list *was* clean; **two of this run's four new brackets moved ~75% of the way to a branch inside
   37 minutes of live trading** (`LHX` ≈ −3.00pp vs `S97`'s −4.0pp; `EW{COHR,LITE}` ≈ +3.75pp vs `S96`'s
   +5.0pp). ⇒ new dig `D282`.

  `M719` shows the basket improves **+4.32pp on 60 days** with `LHX` removed, so the verdict does not
  depend on the leg that has since had a negative issuer event.

  `theme_age` **caps its own age readout at ">=90"** ⇒ 🟢FRESH is **structurally unreachable** for any
  theme older than 90 days (`M714`).

## Added by the 2026-08-18 `industry_kr` run — §5 재논증 (append-only)

> 이 블록은 **회수(retraction)가 아니라 재논증(re-argument)**이다. §5 규칙: *「회수된 항목과 일치하는 주장은
> 버리거나, **옛것을 지명하는 새 측정으로 재논증**한다」.* 아래가 그 형식이다.

| # | 대상 | 새 측정 | 날짜 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **R77-REARGUED** | **`R77`(2026-08-17 `industry_US` 등록) — 「뉴스 다리는 분 단위로 명멸한다」를 회수하고 「실패는 전송이 아니라 전수 스윕이라는 사용 패턴에 붙는다」로 교정한 건** | **회수의 근거였던 측정이 계수 오류였다.** `flow_read.news_velocity()` 는 **실패해도 `None` 이 아니라 dict** 를 돌려준다(`{'velocity': None, 'note': '뉴스 API 응답 실패 + 로컬 FTS 색인 없음'}`). dict-not-None 으로 세면 **40/40 = 100%**, `velocity is not None` 로 세면 **0/40 = 0.0%**. **어제 KR PREFLIGHT 도 같은 자리에서 같은 오류로 「40/40 · 파이프실패 0.0%」를 적었다.** ★ **그리고 원래 가설을 오늘 직접 시계열로 쟀다** — 동일 argv, **2초 간격 90초 42회, 부하 0**: `09:31:46 X×29(61초 연속 실패) → 09:32:48 OK×13(29초 연속 성공)`, **13/42 = 31.0%**. R77 이 요구한 반증(*"명멸하는 다리라면 80요청 중 일부는 떨어졌어야 한다"*)의 정확한 관측이며, **떨어진 게 아니라 통째로 꺼졌다 켜졌다.** ⇒ **「수십 초 블록 명멸」은 살아 있고, R77 의 교정 진단은 틀린 게 아니라 불완전하다** — 같은 날 전수 스윕의 성공 48건은 **최장 연속런 2 · 단발 44개**로 블록이 아니었다. **두 패턴이 함께 존재한다.** ⚠ **[측정 n=1 세션]**. US 쪽 22:14 측정이 같은 계수 결함을 공유하는지는 KR 에서 확인 불가이며, **오늘 US 필터런 10종목은 `vel_coverage 1.0` 로 진짜 velocity 를 받았다**(US 경로가 늘 죽어 있다는 뜻은 아니다) | 2026-08-18 |

### ⚠ 이 런이 스스로 잡은 오류 2건 (회수 아님 — R 번호 없음)

1. **dig ID 3-grep 실패.** `MACRO_REPORT §H` 가 *「기존 최고 D272-KR ⇒ 이 런은 D273-KR~D275-KR」* 이라고 적었는데,
   `handoff/RESEARCH.md:2362` 이 **08-17 런이 D262-KR…D274-KR 를 이미 가져갔다**고 명시한다.
   **「3-grep at WRITE time 했다」고 적고 실제로는 좁게 봤다.** ⇒ 범위를 **`D275-KR ~ D277-KR`** 로 정정하고
   **정정 사실을 원문에 남겼다**(D48). 규칙이 있어도 실행이 안 되면 같은 일이 난다는 표본.
2. **오늘 이 런의 첫 라이브러리 프로브가 「40/40 = 100%」를 출력했다** — 같은 런의 다음 명령이 **0/40** 으로 반증했다.
   **한 줄 바꿔 세었더니 100% 가 0% 가 됐다.** 위 R77-REARGUED 는 이 자기반증에서 나왔다.

## Added by the 2026-08-19 `industry_kr` run — §5 재논증 (append-only)

> **회수가 아니라 재논증이다.** §5 규칙: *「회수된 항목과 일치하는 주장은 버리거나, **옛것을 지명하는 새 측정으로 재논증**한다」.*
> ⚠ **ID 3-grep(WRITE 시점)**: 현행 최고 `R78`(2026-08-17 등록) · `M723` · `D277-KR` · `S63-KR` ⇒ 이 블록은 **R79**.

| # | 대상 | 새 측정 | 날짜 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **R79-REARGUED** | **`R77`(08-17 US 등록) → `R77-REARGUED`(08-18 KR) 계보의 3차 교정.** 08-18 KR 런은 *"블록 명멸이 살아 있다"* 며 R77 의 rate-limit 진단을 되돌렸다. **오늘 그 되돌림을 다시 내리고 「~50콜/~2분 창 쿼터 고갈」로 확정한다 — R77 의 원래 진단이 옳았고 08-18 이 과잉교정이었다.** | **세 독립 프로브가 같은 숫자를 가리킨다.** ① **유휴 42회 = 42/42 100%**(08-18 은 13/42 = 31.0%) ⇒ 「명멸」 가설은 오늘 조건에서 부정됨. ② **버스트 140회 = `[('1',50),('0',90)]`** — 정확히 50콜 성공 후 51번째부터 90연속 실패, 끊김 0. ③ **전수 스윕에서 velocity 가 산 51종목의 유니버스 인덱스 = `[0,1,…,50]` 단일 연속런**(최장런 51 · 단발 0). ④ **회복 프로브: 잠금 후 +77.2초에 OK.** ⇒ 스윕은 종목당 2콜 × 807 ≈ **1,614콜**을 5분에 던져 **첫 51종목에서 쿼터를 소진**하고 나머지 780종목은 **구조적으로 0**이다. **간헐 장애가 아니라 결정론** — 같은 조건이면 매 런 같은 첫 51종목만 산다. 전수를 살리려면 **≈64분**. ⚠ **08-18 의 관측(부하 0에서 69% 실패)이 틀린 게 아니라 그날 터널이 실제로 불안정했던 것**이고, 오늘처럼 터널이 깨끗하면 고갈 하나로 전부 설명된다. **[측정 n=1 세션, 그러나 3중 확증]** | 2026-08-19 |

### ⚠ 이 런이 스스로 잡은 오류 1건 (회수 아님 — R 번호 없음)

★★★ **개장 전 선물 −5% 를 「계기 오류」로 판정했는데, 절반이 틀렸다.**
`MACRO §A-2b` 는 08:50 `--futboard` 의 근월물 **등락 −5.00% · 베이시스 −5.5%** 를 **둘 다** 스테일 호가로 판정하고
반증 근거로 *"간밤 US 는 −0.69%/−1.33% 였다"* 를 썼다. **09:24 정규장 재조회(거래량 20,331):**
**베이시스 −0.27% ⇒ 베이시스 진단은 맞았다**(stale 이론가가 만든 허상). **그러나 등락률은 −6.61% 로 더 벌어졌고**,
라이브가 확정했다 — `069500` **−6.97%** · `005930` **−7.45%** · `000660` **−9.21%**(09:24:17, 장중 24분).
⇒ **KOSPI200 은 오늘 실제로 ~7% 갭다운했다. 선물은 옳았다.**
**무엇을 틀렸나**: 한 표의 **서로 다른 두 숫자(베이시스 · 등락률)를 "이 표의 −5%" 로 뭉쳐 한 판정에 묶었다.**
그리고 **"US 가 −0.69% 였다" 는 KR 갭의 반증으로 부적합**하다 — KR 은 자기 갭을 낼 수 있다.
⚠ **M-79 의 안티시그널 ②도 잘못 겨냥돼 있었다** — 하중은 등락률에 있었는데 브래킷은 베이시스에 걸었고,
**그래서 안티시그널이 미발화한 채 명제의 절반이 틀릴 수 있었다.**
✅ **영향 범위는 좁다**: 이 런의 모든 판정은 08-18 정착 종가로 만들어졌고 **선물 −5% 를 어떤 명제의 근거로도 쓰지 않았다**
(오류로 판정해 배제했으므로). **틀린 판정이 결론을 오염시키지 않은 이유는 운이 아니라, 의심스러운 계기를 배제한 쪽으로 틀렸기 때문이다.**
원문은 지우지 않았다(D48) — `MACRO_REPORT §K` · `BET_SHEET §ALPHA-3`.

It asserts that **the placement of the surviving calls is not stable across runs or markets, so the KR reason must not be imported** (`W1`). ★ New datum the KR desk did not have: the failure surfaces at the **TLS handshake** (`SSLEOFError: EOF occurred in violation of protocol`), identically under `urllib` default context, `urllib` unverified context and `requests` — **not** an HTTP 429. A quota rejection returns a status line; this closes the socket mid-handshake. | 2026-08-19 |
| **R81** | **`M25`** — *"the US 🟢 tag is a **volume-surge** count, not a flow count: with `velocity` dead, 🟢 requires `OBV 매집 ∧ RS20>0 ∧ vol_surge ≥1.2`"* — carried into this run's own `HANDOVER §1` and repeated in `SWEEP_READ §1`. | **This run's own board, measured across all 8 greens.** Only **3 clear `vol_surge` ≥1.2** (`KKR` 1.32 · `LITE` 1.25 · `CSCO` 1.43). **Five carry `vol_surge` well below the bar and a live `velocity`** — `RTX` **0.83/1.55** · `WMT` **0.83/1.35** · `ORCL` 0.76/1.23 · `MA` 0.84/1.35 · `BAC` 0.81/1.49 — i.e. their 🟢 rests on **the axis PREFLIGHT G1 revoked**. Cross-check from the other side: **9 names in the universe clear `vol_surge` ≥1.2 and 4 of them are 🟡, 2 are 🔴** (`COHR` 1.70 🟡 · `MNST` 1.62 🟡 · `SNDK` 1.25 🟡 · `EBAY` 1.20 🟡 · `WEC` 1.45 🔴 · `NKE` 1.40 🔴). ⇒ `flow_tag` does **not** take the run-level axis set, while `flow_score` does (`D225-KR`). 🚨 **`R78` reproduced on the run that claimed to have pre-empted it**: `HANDOVER §1` wrote *"`R78`'s lesson is pre-empted this run: no stage may promote on a tag today"*, and then `SWEEP_READ §1`, `SECTOR_ROTATION §2b`/§3 and `EVENT_ALPHA` Card 8 each used the tag. ★ **What survives**: every underlying `obv_norm`, `rs20` and `flow_score` — no verdict moved on this retraction alone, because each claim's price leg carried it unaided. **What is withdrawn**: the tag as evidence, the new-🟢 count (`RTX`, `WMT` — both velocity-derived), and shortlist membership as a quality signal. | 2026-08-19 |
| **R82** | **This run's own `SECTOR_ROTATION §2b` promotion of Consumer Staples UW− → UW**, carried by *"Δ +0.325 — the largest two-session improvement on the board."* | **The Δ is 77.7% rolling-window roll-off.** `rs20` is a rolling 20-session excess vs `SPY`; advancing `asof` 08-14 → 08-18 adds two sessions at the front **and drops two off the back**, and nothing in the pipeline separates them. Decomposed across all 11 sectors (EW ΔRS20 = earned + roll-off): **Staples +2.52 = +0.57 earned + 1.96 roll-off**; corrected, its earned improvement ranks **6th of 11, not 1st of 11**. The desk over-rated the sector it promoted and under-rated the one it held. ⚠ **Health Care's N→N+ is NOT retracted**: 40.2% roll-off but **+1.55pp earned, 2nd of 11 corrected**, and its three other legs (`eqflow` above `wflow`; `ex_top1` higher than headline; β-adjusted **+1.158 vs `SPY`**) use no Δ at all. ⚠ `S80` and `S100` remain ARMED and still settle — a withdrawn verdict does not release the desk from scoring what it registered. | 2026-08-19 |

The standing regime call — *"memory is a price-cycle industry where the LEVEL is tight while the RATE OF CHANGE
decelerates"* — has been carried as `[inferred]` and barred from use as evidence. **This run tied both of its legs
to a primary source for the first time** (`M764` below). It is proposed for promotion to `[measured]`; the promotion

citable as measurements; the inference drawn from them is still an inference.**

### ⚠ 이 런이 스스로 잡은 오류 **3건** (회수 아님 — R 번호 없음, D48 대로 append)

1. ★★ **`MACRO §G` 가 `수주잔고` 8.40배를 산업재(건설·조선·방산)의 서사로 적었고, 같은 런의 `SECTOR_DEEP_INDU` 가 반박했다.**
   `fts search 수주잔고 --days 7 --scope domestic` = **97건**이고 BM25 상위가 **반도체 장비**다
   (zdnet 08-21: 원익IPS 2,982→6,346억 **2.13배** · 테스 972→2,069억 2.13배 · 주성엔지니어링 885→2,144억 **2.42배** · 파크시스템스 636→1,125억 1.77배).
   건설 항목은 **현대건설×테라파워 원전 8기 EPC 우선권(08-18)** 한 건뿐이다. **MACRO 문장은 지우지 않고 DEEP 에 정정을 붙였다.**
2. ★★ **`SECTOR_DEEP_INDU §4` 가 「028050 은 07-01 이후 신규 수주 공시 0건(52일)」을 관측으로 적었고, 다음 스테이지에서 반박됐다.**
   `REPORT/COMPANY_SCOREBOARD.md`(08-21) 가 이미 분해해 놓았다 — **단일계약 공시 임계(약 4,500억)의 산물**이고
   **총 수주잔고 16.07 → 20.19조(+25.6%) · 커버리지 1.78 → 2.07년**. ⇒ **「수주가 안 온다」가 아니라 「임계 아래는 공시되지 않는다」.**
   ★ **이것은 위 `R91` 과 같은 형태다** — 하나는 **흐름 게이트(서지 1.2)** 가, 하나는 **공시 임계(4,500억)** 가 같은 섹터를 「비어 있음」으로 보이게 만든다. **두 축에서 독립 재현됐다.**
3. ★ **이 런이 아침에 047040 을 「중앙값 회복」으로 부활시켰는데, 같은 시리즈를 자기 분포에 대고 다시 재니 「자기 역사상 최고」였다.**
   `margin_history 047040 --quarterly`: **2026Q1 18.6% = 분기 42기 자체 최고 · 2026Q2 17.1% = 2위**(분기 중앙값 10.5%).
   ⇒ **부활 판정은 유지한다**(등록 조건이 「중앙값 9.7% 회복」이었고 명백히 충족됐다 — 조건을 사후에 바꾸지 않는다).
   **바뀐 것은 해석이다**: 그것은 **렌즈 L2(피크마진 함정)의 정확한 정의**이며, 수급은 **개인 흡수**(외 −315.8 / 개 +11.7만), rs60 **−32.7**.

### ⚠ 상태 변경 1건 — 회수가 아니라 **유보 사유의 소진**
standing item: *"`vol_surge` 는 두 지평에서 부호가 일치하는 유일한 축이고 `sector_flow` 의 녹색 판정은 여전히 그것에 양(+)의 가중을 준다 — **Bonferroni 를 통과하기 전에는 게이트를 뒤집지 않는다(P4)**."*
⇒ **오늘 두 지평 모두 통과했다**(h=1 t −3.86 / n_eff 34.0 · h=5 t −3.38 / n_eff 5.4, **둘 다 n_eff >= 4**).
**게이트 변경 자체는 여전히 사람 결정(P5)이다. 그러나 「아직 조건 미달」이라는 사유는 더 쓸 수 없다** — 상태는 **「조건 충족 · 사람 결정 대기」**로 바뀐다.

| **R93** | **"Kevin Warsh's Jackson Hole debut is TODAY, 2026-08-21"** — written by the 2026-08-21 `industry_US` run in its MACRO headline, its asof chain and the registration text of `S108`; and the conclusion drawn from it, **"`D18`/`D288-KR`'s most expensive instance yet: `catalyst_calendar` missed a Fed-chair speech at D-0."** That run then **overwrote its own HANDOVER §6 sentence, *"No binary lands ≤48h from this run,"*** describing the overwrite as a self-correction. | **A body-level date string, measured 2026-08-22, `--scope foreign`.** `economictimes` (08-22), verbatim: *"the **August 27 to August 29 event** in Jackson Hole."* Corroborated in future tense by five independent sources dated 08-21: `fxstreet` **10:43 ET** — *"**Forecasting the upcoming week**: Warsh's Jackson Hole debut…"* and *"Warsh's Jackson Hole speech **will** focus on…"*; `wsj` *"**Week Ahead** for FX, Bonds: Warsh Speech at Jackson Hole"*; `KITCO` *"Gold looks to PCE inflation, Jackson Hole for direction **next week**"*; `seekingalpha` *"**Catalyst Watch**: … Warsh at Jackson Hole"*; `reuters`/`investing_en` *"Nvidia earnings, Jackson Hole to test pillars of stock rally"*. ⇒ **The date was wrong by six days.** ★★★ **And the second-order retraction is the more valuable one: `catalyst_calendar` did NOT miss a D-0 binary — there was no D-0 binary. The calendar was right and the run overrode it on a Week-Ahead headline, replacing a TRUE HANDOVER sentence with a false one.** This desk has spent runs building machinery to catch *"the calendar missed a dated event"*; that machinery, applied under time pressure, **manufactured an event that did not exist** — a **false-positive** direction never before logged in this ledger. ★ **What survives: `D18` is still real.** `catalyst_calendar --days 10`, run 2026-08-22, carries **zero rows for Jackson Hole 08-27→08-29** while listing July PCE 08-28, `NVDA` 08-26, `FRO` 08-28, MSCI 08-31. **The gap is genuine; yesterday's dating of it was not.** ⚠ **`S108` is NOT withdrawn and NOT re-frozen** (`D242`): its window is 08-20 → 08-25 and Jackson Hole is 08-27→29, so **it settles as registered while measuring nothing about the event it was built for** | 2026-08-22 |

### ⚠ What this run caught in ITSELF — **1 in-run self-correction (NOT a retraction, no R number)**

★ **The run's own PREFLIGHT over-reached and the ledger audit caught it two stages later.** The first
draft of `PREFLIGHT_US` gate G1 rule 3 revoked `breadth`/`green`/`red` **outright**, on a real
measurement (9 greens vs 6 on one identical price frame). **A `missed_ledger` row (`CBRE`) whose
condition reads *"enters the green set on an admissible (non-velocity) axis"* forced a source read of
`module_flow/_synthesize.py:10-49`**, which shows that with `vel = None` the green term
`(vel or 0) >= 1.2` and the red term `(vel is not None and vel < 0.7)` are **both False** ⇒ the tag
collapses to a pure 3-axis price function. **The persisted sweep has `velocity: null` on all 299
names, so today's tags were provably velocity-free and citable.** The rule was narrowed to
partial-coverage runs, **the original wording was kept beside the correction**, and the rights tables
were re-propagated. ⇒ **the audit step found the error in the gate step, which is the productive
direction for that dependency to run.**

### §6 open contradictions — **`C11` is RESOLVED and moves OUT** (carried since 2026-08-19)

**`C11` was**: *"the two OBV instruments disagree, and the desk does not yet know whether it is method
or clock."* **Resolved 2026-08-22, and only a market-closed session could have resolved it.**
- **Test**: US cash closed all day ⇒ both instruments read **the same settled 2026-08-21 bar with zero
  clock difference**. Under the clock hypothesis they had to agree. **5 of 5 still disagreed** —
  `ANET` 매집 +0.152 vs 분배 −27% · `NUE` +0.148 vs −71% · `RTX` +0.023 vs −71% · `PYPL` +0.138 vs
  −50% · `CRWD` −0.058 vs **누적 +35%** (inverting the other way). ⇒ **clock hypothesis REFUTED.**
- **Mechanism, from both sources**: sweep (`module_flow/_price_flow.py:28-32`) builds a **cumulative**
  OBV (`.cumsum()`) and takes `obv[-1] − obv[-21]` ÷ 20d mean volume, threshold ±0.08 — a **LEVEL**.
  `module_chart` (`_metadata.py:125-131`) builds a **rolling-20** OBV (`.rolling(20).sum()`) and takes
  `obv[-1] − obv[-10]` ÷ the rolling series' own range, threshold ±0.15 — a **RATE OF CHANGE**.
  **The chart's quantity is approximately the derivative of the sweep's.**
- ⇒ **METHOD, and further: not a contradiction at all once the definitions are read.** A name
  accumulating over 20 sessions whose rolling window has shrunk for 10 reads 매집 on one and 분배 on
  the other, **correctly, at the same instant**. ✅ **DEEP-MATR's 12-of-12 reconstruction is NOT
  overturned** — it rebuilt OBV using the *sweep's* definition and agreed with the sweep, exactly as
  it should; only its *interpretation* (clock artifact) is refuted.
- **The standing constraint is REPLACED, not lifted**: ✅ naming the *clock* is no longer required;
  🚫 **naming the INSTRUMENT and the QUANTITY is now mandatory** — *"sweep `obv_norm`, 20-session
  level"* or *"`module_chart`, 10-session slope of the rolling-20."* **Calling both "OBV" is what
  created a phantom contradiction that survived three runs.** ⚠ **`D6` untouched — this does not
  promote OBV from grade C.**
- ★ **Same-run confirmation of the mechanism**: the two instruments **AGREED** on `MPC` (+0.318 /
  누적 +48%), `MRK` (+0.370 / +32%) and `COIN` (+0.289 / +96%) — all three accumulating **and**
  accelerating — and **disagreed only on `RTX` and `NVDA`, the two decelerating names, both held.**
- ★★ And the finding rhymes with §1: **a level and its second derivative pointing opposite ways is not
  a contradiction — it is the shape of a decelerating cycle.** The desk wrote that sentence about
  memory prices in July and then spent three runs failing to apply it to its own instrumentation.

### ⚠ 이 런이 스스로 잡은 오류 **3건** (회수 아님 — R 번호 없음, `D48` 대로 append)

1. 🚨🚨 **`SECTOR_DEEP_INDU §3-c` 가 두 OBV 구현의 부호 불일치를 「이 런이 새로 잡은 계기 불일치」로 적고 dig 후보로 올렸다. 어제 이미 해소된 항목이다.**
   **08-22 US 런이 `C11` 을 해소해 §6 에서 내보냈고**(`M796`: 스윕은 **레벨**(누적 OBV, `[-1]−[-21]`, ÷vol20, ±0.08), `module_chart` 는
   **변화율**(rolling20, `[-1]−[-10]`, ÷자기 레인지, ±0.15) ⇒ **방법이지 모순이 아니다**), **그 블록은 이 런이 HANDOVER 에서 읽은 스파인 안에 있었다.**
   ⇒ **정정을 파일 말미에 append 했고 원문은 지우지 않았다.** **살아남는 것은 관측값과 처방**(「어느 구현·어느 창인지 병기」)이고,
   ★ **`C11` 의 해소가 그 처방을 더 유용하게 만든다** — 「레벨 양(+) ∧ 변화율 음(−)」은 **「매집 중이나 매집 속도가 꺾인다」**는 정보다(028050 이 그 형태).
   ⚠ **`D317-KR` 로 등록**: 이 런은 §5(회수)는 읽었는데 **§6 에서 방금 빠져나간 항목**을 확인하지 않았다.
   **08-22 KR 런이 `D306-KR` 에서 똑같은 실수를 했다**(*"dig 를 올리기 전에 이게 이미 결정된 사항인가 확인한다"*) ⇒ **2런 연속 같은 형태.**
2. ★★ **`MACRO §G ③` 이 *"산업재의 서사(`수주잔고` 8.57×)와 가격(−6~−8pp 초과)이 정면으로 갈렸다. 둘 중 하나는 틀렸고 오늘 어느 쪽인지 모른다"* 로 적었고,
   같은 런의 `SECTOR_DEEP_INDU §2` 가 두 스테이지 뒤에 반박했다.** 실측: `fts search 수주잔고 --days 7 --scope domestic` **96건**,
   BM25 상위가 전부 **반도체 장비**(zdnet 08-21 「삼성·SK 증설에 수주잔고 2배 급증」), 건설 항목은 **현대건설×테라파워 EPC 우선권(08-18) 1건**뿐.
   ⇒ **3방향 모순은 실은 1방향이었다** — 흐름 다리는 계기 산물(`R91`), 서사 다리는 **다른 산업의 뉴스**였다. **MACRO 문장은 지우지 않고 DEEP 에 정정을 붙였다.**
3. ★ **`BET §1-c` 가 `COMPANY_SCOREBOARD`(08-21)의 숫자 하나를 정정했다** — 행이 *"028050 08-21 **−5.2%**"* 로 적었으나
   **정착 실측은 48,050 → 45,150 = −6.035%**(yfinance `auto_adjust=False`). ⇒ **−5.2% 는 미정착봉(장중) 값이다**(`D74`).
   **레그의 방향과 「프레임 공유」 판정은 바뀌지 않지만 크기가 0.8pp 다르다.** **행을 덮어쓰지 않고 시트에 정정을 붙였다.**

### ⚠ 상태 변경 1건 — 회수가 아니라 **차트 판정의 하향**(`M168` 컨벤션)
**028050 삼성E&A**: 08-21 캐리는 *"chart **CONFIRMED-TURN**, bull stack 5>20>60>120, trigger **48,650**"* 였다.
오늘 `module_chart --read` 실측은 **턴-판정 `NEUTRAL/CHOP`**, **강세스택은 유지되나 가격이 4개 MA 전부 아래**, **트리거 45,642**(−6.2%), 스탑 **39,750**(불변).
**킬이 발화한 것이 아니라 상태가 내려간 것**이므로 회수하지 않고 **STALE 로 표시한다.**

## §6 열린 모순 — **`C14` 신규 등록** (해결하지 않고 의도적으로 운반)

| id | 모순 | 왜 한쪽을 고르지 않는가 |
|---|---|---|
| **C14** | **IT/전기·전자에서 C급 폭 지표와 A급 수급이 정반대를 가리키고, 두 축 다 이 데스크가 인정한 축이다.** C급: `eqflow` **−0.174**(28섹터 중 3번째로 나쁨) · `ex-top1` −0.409 · **플리퍼 아님** · breadth **0.030** · **🟢2:🔴19 = 보드 최악 비율** · 이항 z **−2.29**. A급: **005930 뒤반 10세션 외 +883.5만 ∧ 기 +351.3만**(`R95`) · 08-21 단일 세션 외 +156.7 ∧ 기 +130.7. | **ROTATION 이 강등 시도를 A급 반증으로 기각했고**(§2-c ②), **그 A급 관측은 오늘 처음 만들어진 계기(앞반/뒤반 분할)이며 재현된 적이 없다.** ⇒ **한쪽을 고르면 (i) 재현 0회짜리 새 계기를 등급 근거로 승격시키거나 (ii) 부호가 음으로 유의한 축(`vol_surge`)이 만든 breadth 를 등급 근거로 쓰게 된다.** **둘 다 이 데스크가 스스로 금지한 것이다.** 해소는 **측정**으로만 — `S67-KR`(2026-09-04)이 leg 1 로 외국인 20일 부호를 이미 묻고 있고, **분할 계기의 재현 횟수가 관측 대상**이다 |

It was inferred from one aggregate (🟢 9 → 6) because the 08-22 run **did not persist run 1** and wrote *"the three names are unrecoverable."* | **The same experiment, run today with BOTH outputs persisted.** Two sweeps four minutes apart on one identical price frame (`asof` 2026-08-21): **all 299 `flow_score`s and all 11 sector `wflow`s identical, 6 TAGS differ** — `MRVL` 🟢→🟡 · `MA` 🟢→🟡 · `CVX` 🟢→🟡 · `MS` 🔴→🟡 · `JPM` 🔴→🟡 · `WMT` 🟡→🔴. **Three up, three down; five of six move AWAY from neutral.** Run 1 carried **one extra 🔴 as well as three extra 🟢** (72 vs 71). ⇒ **The bias is DISPERSION, not optimism.** ★ **And the remedy the 08-22 run proposed — "discount the greens" — would have left `JPM` and `MS` mis-tagged 🔴.** An upward bias is correctable by a one-directional haircut; a dispersion bias is not correctable at all. ★ **What SURVIVES**: the *uniform-vs-selective* half of `M795` is untouched and better evidenced — zero coverage is missing-completely-at-random, partial coverage is missing conditional on bucket membership, so **a zero-coverage tag layer is the only citable one**. ★ **Ledger consequence nobody had counted**: the persisted sweeps of **08-18 (16.72%) · 08-19 (17.06%) · 08-20 (16.39%) · 08-21 (16.72%)** all carry partial-coverage tag layers; **only 08-22 and 08-23 are clean.** The repair is to **re-derive breadth from the score column** (which never sees `vel`), not to haircut greens | 08-22 `PREFLIGHT_US.md` §3 · `HANDOVER.md` §0 · the 08-22 asof chain (`M795`) | 2026-08-23 |

### ⚠ What this run caught in ITSELF — **3 in-run self-corrections (NOT retractions, no R numbers)**

1. 🚨🚨 **`MACRO §A-3` first computed CPI YoY POSITIONALLY and got the inflation rate 0.24–0.32pp TOO HIGH.**
   `obs[-13]` returned **+3.54% headline / +2.79% core**; the date-matched values are **+3.30% / +2.47%**.
   **Cause: `CPIAUCSL`, `CPILFESL` and `UNRATE` are MISSING the 2025-10-01 observation** (the series jumps
   2025-09-01 → 2025-11-01), so the thirteenth element back is **August 2025, not July 2025**.
   **It was caught only because the 08-22 report carried +3.30%/+2.47% and the two disagreed.**
   ⇒ Every figure in the published table is date-matched, the error is written into the report rather than
   edited away, and the defect is registered as **`D326`**. ★ **A positional YoY on a FRED monthly series is
   unsafe in the direction that makes inflation look worse.**
2. ★★ **`ROTATION §2` attempt 3 cited *"breadth 0.000 of 28 names"* as evidence to demote Consumer
   Discretionary, and `SWEEP §3` called Communication Services *"structurally unreadable"*. Both readings
   were wrong, and `DEEP-HLTH` refuted them two stages later** by reading the source:
   **`sector_flow.py:342` — `"breadth": round(greens / len(names), 2)`.** `breadth` **is the 🟢 rate**, and
   🟢 requires `vol_surge ≥ 1.2`. DISC's true participation (`OBV 매집 ∧ rs20>0`) is **39.3%**;
   **COMM's is 66.7%, the highest on the board.** ⇒ **The verdicts do not change** (the demotion was
   declined anyway) **but the reasoning that supported them did.** Registered as **`D325`**.
3. ★★ **`EVENT_ALPHA` Card 2 wrote *"a rate-of-change inflection off a bad LEVEL"* about the memory complex,
   and DRIFT refuted it from price.** Settled 08-21: **`MU` +115.6% off its 90-day low and −20.3% from its
   90-day high · `SNDK` +79.0% / −31.6% · `LRCX` +26.2% / −27.5%** (`M839`). **"Bad level" was true of the
   relative FLOW score and false of the price.** ⇒ **The complex is a 20–32% drawdown inside a very large
   uptrend, not a base** — which makes the `L2` peak-margin caution apply MORE, not less. **The card's cell
   verdict (STORY-ONLY, not handed to BET) and its four miss-ledger rows stand unchanged** (`D242`).

### ⚠ One inherited claim UPGRADED rather than retracted
**`F1` — *"🟢LIVE fired 0 times in 8 consecutive runs; a gate that never fires looks identical to a
universe with nothing in it"* — is RESOLVED on the US side, and the resolution is that the gate WORKS.**
In one session on one instrument, `theme-age --scope foreign` returned **`Treasury buyback` 🟢FRESH

when a theme is genuinely new. The zero on equity theses is ARITHMETIC, not instrument.**

separate market (`W1`).

| **`R97`** | **「`D303-KR`(000660 08-14 파생상품거래손실 공시 미개봉)를 이 런이 4런 만에 처음 열었다」** — 오늘 `HANDOVER §★③` · `MACRO §D-4` · `EVENT_ALPHA 카드 1` 세 파일이 이것을 **이 런의 최초 발견**으로 적었다 | **같은 런의 `BET` 스테이지가 `REPORT/COMPANY_SCOREBOARD.md` 를 열자마자 반증됐다.** `company_batch`(2026-08-21, run 1)의 **000660 행이 그 공시를 이미 읽었고 우리보다 깊었다** — 우리는 공시 본문 1건이었는데 그 행은 **주석24 · 08-19 자사주 공시 각주 · 자기주식 잔고의 3중 교차**를 했고, **`Clean-Up Call 2026-05 행사 · 잔여 73주`** 로 **재발 불가를 수치로 확정**했으며, **거래상대방이 공시에 없다는 사실까지 `unknown` 으로 명시**했다. 그 행은 심지어 *"3.98조짜리 항목에 뉴스축 0건"* 이라는, 오늘 우리가 「독립 재현」이라 부른 관측까지 갖고 있었다 | 2026-08-24 `industry_kr` 의 HANDOVER · MACRO · EVENT_ALPHA | 2026-08-24 |

**★ 이 회수가 남기는 것 — 살아남는 절반은 여전히 크다**
- **회수되는 것은 「최초」라는 주장 하나**다. **공시의 내용 자체는 두 데스크가 독립적으로 같은 결론에 도달했다**(`M845`).
- ★ **그리고 오늘 이 런이 스코어보드에 **없던** 것을 하나 더했다**: **`"자사주" "소각"` 본문 128건 · `"하이닉스" "소각"` 80건**으로
  **einfomax 08-19 「'40조 자사주 매입' 하이닉스 당장 내일부터 산다…1조원 장내 매수 신청」** 과
  **08-20 「[하이닉스株 소각 파급] "ADR 조달분 되사는 셈"」** 을 뉴스 본문에서 끌어냈고,
  그것으로 **08-20 KOSPI +5.89% 의 원인을 특정**했다(`M848`). **스코어보드는 그 뉴스 다리를 갖고 있지 않았다.**
- 🚨 **절차 결함이 이 회수의 진짜 내용이다**: **HANDOVER 가 `module_report_tags show` 로 `COMPANY_SCOREBOARD.md` 의
  존재를 봤고 본문을 열지 않았다.** ⇒ **`D317-KR`·`D306-KR` 과 같은 클래스의 3런 연속 재현**이고,
  이번엔 「§6 에서 나간 항목」이 아니라 **「`REPORT/` 에 있는 완성 파일」**이다.
  ⇒ **긍정형 처방 → `D330-KR`.**

### ⚠ 이 런이 자기 자신에게서 잡은 것 — **자기수정 3건 (회수 아님, R 번호 안 붙임)**

1. 🚨🚨 **`PREFLIGHT §G3` 가 *"✅ 대체재: `eqflow`·`breadth` 는 살아 있다"* 라고 적었고, 같은 런의 `MACRO §D-3` 가 반증했다.**
   상속한 `D325`(US 08-23)를 KR 소스에 대보니 `sector_flow.py:342` 가 `greens/len(names)` 이고 🟢 는
   `_synthesize.py:16` 의 `vol_surge>=1.2` 를 지난다 ⇒ **`breadth` 는 폭이 아니라 🟢율**이고, **그 문지기는
   `ic_ledger`(KR)가 두 지평 Bonferroni 로 부호 음(−)을 잡은 축**이다. **806종 전수 괴리 +26.4pp**(`M844`).
   **PREFLIGHT 문장을 고치지 않고 MACRO 에 반박을 붙였고, ROTATION 이 「근거 철회 1건」으로 집행했다.**
2. 🚨 **`EVENT_ALPHA` 가 `S74`/UAL 을 재는 첫 조회에서 `fts search "compensation Iran"` → 0건을 얻어
   그것을 「보상 프레임워크 없음」의 근거로 미스원장 노트에 적었다.** 상속한 `D324` 를 적용해 재실행하니
   **`"compensation" AND "Iran"` 52건 · `"reopen" AND "Hormuz"` 164건**이었다.
   **결론(브랜치 A 미발화)은 164건 분모 위에서 살았지만 처음 제출한 증거는 계기가 만든 침묵이었다.**
   **원장 행을 덮어쓰지 않고 정정 노트를 append 했다.**
3. 🚨 **`MACRO §D-2 ③` 가 *"소각 서사는 뉴스에 없고 공시에만 있다"* 라고 썼고(`자사주소각` 총 1건 · 0.00× 🔴),
   `EVENT_ALPHA §0` 이 같은 런에서 반증했다** — **`"자사주" "소각"` 인용 병치 검색은 128건**이다.
   원인은 **`M846`**: **KR trigram 색인에서 `AND` 연산자가 결과를 0으로 만든다**(`--scope foreign` 에서는 작동).
   **MACRO 를 고치지 않고 EVENT_ALPHA 에 반박을 붙였다.**

### ⚠ 상속한 진단 1건이 **회수가 아니라 좁혀졌다** — `D309`

`D309` 는 *"FRED 는 주말에 게시하지 않으므로 금요일 정산 브래킷을 주말 데스크가 구조적으로 못 읽는다"* 였고,
**08-22·08-23 의 5회 관측은 전부 주말이라 두 설명이 구분되지 않았다.**
**오늘은 월요일이고, 같은 FRED 에서 `T10YIE`(2.34)와 `RRPONTSYD`(0.200)는 08-21 을 갖고 있는데
`DGS2`/`DGS10`/`DGS30`/`DFII10`/`OAS 2종`/`VIXCLS`/`DFF` 는 08-20 이 마지막이다**(2경로 독립 조회, `M840`).
⇒ **정정판: 주말이 아니라 시리즈별 게시 래그이고, H.15 계열이 `T10YIE` 보다 최소 1영업일 늦다.**
🚫 **앞선 5회 관측을 지우지 않는다** — 그 날들은 두 설명을 구분할 수 없는 날이었고, **월요일 관측 1회가 처음 구분했다.**

## §6 열린 모순 — **`C15` 신규 등록** (해결하지 않고 의도적으로 운반)

| id | 모순 | 왜 한쪽을 고르지 않는가 |
|---|---|---|
| **C15** | **`breadth`(🟢율)와 `participation`(OBV매집 ∧ rs20>0)이 KR 섹터 순위를 크게 다르게 매기는데, 두 지표의 구성 축이 전부 이 데스크 자신의 원장에서 약하다.** `breadth` 쪽: 문지기 `vol_surge` 가 **h=1 t(NW) −3.86/n_eff 34.0 · h=5 −3.38/n_eff 5.4 = 두 지평 Bonferroni 통과, 부호 음(−)**. `participation` 쪽: `obv_norm` h=5 t **−2.08(Bonferroni 미통과)** · `rs20` h=5 t **−1.49(구분 불가)**. 괴리 실측 **+26.4pp**(806종), 순위 이동 **건설 공동꼴찌→7위 · 제약 22위→5위**. | **한쪽을 고르면 (i) 부호가 음으로 유의한 축이 만든 지표를 계속 쓰거나 (ii) 「구분 불가」 축 둘로 갈아타게 된다. 둘 다 이 데스크가 스스로 금지한 것이다.** ⇒ **오늘의 처리는 「`breadth` 를 폭으로 부르지 않는다 + `participation` 을 수준이 아니라 명단으로만 인용한다」**이고, **해소는 측정으로만** — `ic_ledger` 에 `participation` 축을 적립해 `n_eff>=4` 에서 부호를 보는 것이 유일한 경로다(`M-101` 안티시그널 ①). ★ **그리고 오늘 `M850` 이 그 명단의 불안정성을 이미 쟀다** — 건설 4창 안정성(교/합) **0.429**, 이탈 트리거가 `rs20` **−0.4pp** 였다 |

> ⚠ **Write mode append-only** — `D165` pre-commitment. Nothing above this line was read into memory
> and rewritten.

> **0 hit** in all three. Current highest **`R97`** (2026-08-24 `industry_kr`, this morning).
> ⚠ **Language: English** — the US desk's documented practice.

| # | Claim retracted | What killed it | Who held it | Date |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`R98`** | **"The ledger is `market=kr` … the US desk has **no equivalent measurement** and therefore no verdict"** — plus the row count that supported it, **"0 new rows accrued (729 rows, `market=kr`)"** | **One CLI flag.** `scripts/ic_ledger.py:336` sets `--market` with **`default="kr"`** on all three subcommands, and `carryover.md` §3e prints the commands **without** it. Run as `ic_ledger.py score --market us`, the US ledger returns a **15-test table over 33 run-dates (2026-07-13 → 08-20)**. **Two errors, both checkable at the time: (a) 729 was the WHOLE FILE, not the kr subset — today it reads 509 kr + 405 us = 914, of which **185 were accrued by this run**, so **220 US rows were already on disk on 08-23**, ≈15 run-dates, enough for an h=1 verdict; (b) the sign does not merely exist, it INVERTS — `vol_surge` h=1 is `IC +0.0398`, `t(NW) +3.40`, 67% positive in the US against `−0.0454 / −3.86 / 24%` in KR, BOTH Bonferroni-passing** (`M853`, `M854`) | 2026-08-23 `industry_US` HANDOVER §6 | 2026-08-24 |

**★ What SURVIVES the retraction, and it is most of it.** The `W1` refusal itself was **correct** —
importing the KR-measured IC into the US sweep would have been a violation, **and today's US
measurement proves it would also have been wrong in DIRECTION.** What is retracted is the
*"no equivalent measurement"* clause and the row count, **not the discipline that produced them.**
This is the cleanest `W1` confirmation this desk has produced: not *"we declined to transfer"* but
**"we transferred nothing, then measured that the transfer would have been backwards."**
⚠ **The exact pre-accrual date coverage of those 220 rows is UNRECOVERABLE** — the ledger is
append-only with no vintage column. **Stated as unrecoverable rather than estimated (P4).**

## §6 open contradictions — **`C15` ANNOTATED with its US arm (NOT resolved; it widens)**

> `C15` was registered this morning by `industry_kr`: *"`breadth`(🟢율) and `participation` rank KR
> sectors very differently, and BOTH indicators' constituent axes are weak in this desk's own ledger."*
> **The US desk now has the missing half of that table, and it does not agree with the KR half.**

| id | Annotation added 2026-08-24 (`industry_US`) |
|---|---|
| **`C15`** | ★★★ **The US arm inverts the KR arm on the gatekeeper axis.** KR: `vol_surge` h=1 `t(NW) −3.86` / h=5 `−3.38`, **both Bonferroni-passing, NEGATIVE** ⇒ the 🟢 gate selects *against* forward returns. **US: `vol_surge` h=1 `IC +0.0398`, `t(NW) +3.40`, `n_eff 34.0`, 67% of run-days positive — Bonferroni-passing, POSITIVE** (`M854`). ⚠ **US h=5 is `+0.0119 / t +0.44` = indistinguishable, so `D105` (two-horizon agreement) is NOT satisfied for `vol_surge` in the US** — the positive result is **horizon-1 only** and must be quoted with its horizon. ⇒ **Neither market may borrow the other's verdict on this axis (`W1`), and the contradiction is now two-sided rather than one-sided.** ★ **Separately, the US ledger's STRONGEST cell is a different axis and it IS two-horizon consistent: `rs60` h=5 `IC −0.1310`, `t(NW) −5.46`, 7% positive, `n_eff 5.4`, with h=1 the same sign (`−0.0635 / −2.77`)** (`M855`) ⇒ **in the 2026-07-13→08-20 window, high 60-day relative strength ranks AGAINST 1–5-session forward returns in the US top-300, and `rs20`/`rs60` are two of the sweep's four surviving axes.** ⚠ **Regime label required**: that window contains the mid-August drawdown, and `rs60`'s −5.46 is the shape a drawdown produces. ⚠ `n_eff 5.4` clears the bar only just; the h=10 cells (`rs60 −0.2014 / t −3.87`) are **unquotable** at `n_eff 2.1`. 🚫 **No axis was flipped, killed or re-weighted — code changes are a human call (P5).** **The resolution path is unchanged and is now two-market: accrue `participation` as an axis in BOTH ledgers and read the sign at `n_eff ≥ 4`** |

  *"`"자사주" "소각"` 128건인데 단일텀 `자사주소각` 은 1건 0.00×, 그래서 MACRO §D-2 ③ 문장이 같은 런에서 반증됐다."*
  **오늘 아침 HANDOVER 도 MACRO 도 그 문단을 읽지 않았다.** ⇒ **오늘이 더한 것은 ① 배율(9.36×)과 ② 공백변형 대조 5쌍뿐**이고,
  **「오늘 판명됐다」는 신규성 주장은 `MACRO_REPORT §J` 에서 append-only 로 자기정정했다.**
- ⇒ **절차 결함이 이 회수의 진짜 내용이다**: **HANDOVER 의 필수 상속 목록에 직전 런의 `SECTOR_ROTATION.md`
  계측 경고 블록이 없다.** `D330-KR`(스코어보드 본문 미개봉)와 같은 클래스의 **연속 재현**. ⇒ **긍정형 처방 `D347-KR`.**
- ⚠ **이 회수는 같은 런 안에서 한 번 더 좁혀졌다**(아래 자기수정 2) — 결함의 클래스는 **공백**이 아니라
  **「한 개념에 여러 표면형이 있고 데스크가 임의로 하나를 골랐다」**이다.

### ⚠ 이 런이 자기 자신에게서 잡은 것 — **자기수정 3건 (회수 아님, R 번호 안 붙임)**

1. **`EVENT_ALPHA` 의 흐름 태그가 「08-24 정착봉」이 아니었다.** `module_flow` 는 내부에서 `yf.download(period="4mo")`
   를 부르고, **KRX 는 09:00 에 열렸으며 이 파일의 KR 호출은 09:0x~09:5x 에 나갔다** ⇒ **마지막 봉이 08-25 미완봉.**
   실측: `000720.KS` 08-24 105,600 → **08-25 115,650**(거래량 454,359 진행 중) · `069500.KS` 08-24 105,995 → **08-25 103,010**.
   **08-24 컷으로 같은 수식을 재계산해 정정표를 append 했다**(`EVENT_ALPHA §3`) — **17개 이름 중 부호가 뒤집힌 칸 6개**
   (KSS해운 RS20 +8.9→−0.2 · HD한국조선해양 RS60 +5.3→−0.9 · 기아 RS60 +0.7→−1.3 · CJ제일제당 RS20 +1.6→−2.0 ·
   LGD RS20 +1.9→−2.2 · **한화오션 OBV 중립→매집**). ⚠ **카드 평결은 하나도 안 바뀌었고, 원장 노트 두 개가 바뀌었다.**
2. **`M-104` 의 「이 텀 하나」 관측이 같은 런 ALPHA 에서 좁혀졌다.** 같은 개념의 네 표기가 정반대 판정:
   `소비심리` **446건 0.19× 🔴** · **`소비자심리지수` 77건 3.63× 🟡** · `소비자심리` **10건 0.00× 🔴** · `소비자 심리` 6건 2.14×.
   **오늘 CCSI 는 머리층 5매체 사건이었다.** ⚠ **`M-104` 안티시그널 ② 의 문자(「45텀 중 3개 이상에서 **공백** 변형」)는
   미발화** — 오늘 것은 어미 변형이고 45텀 밖이다. **사후에 임계를 넓히지 않았다.**
3. **`SECTOR_ROTATION` 이 HLTH 슬롯 근거로 적은 「흐름·사건·KIS 셋이 같은 방향」 중 셋째가 DEEP 에서 반증됐다.**
   딜 발표일(08-24) **한미약품 외국인 −9.1만/개인 +2.6만**, **한미사이언스 외국인 −40.0만/개인 +28.5만** ⇒
   **쇼트리스트의 `✅진짜손` 라벨과 정반대**다. ⚠ **기관은 두 이름 다 그날에도 순매수** — **세 주체 중 둘이 갈린다.**

### ⚠ 상속한 관측 하나가 **하루 만에 되돌아왔다** — 회수가 아니라 노이즈였다
어제 KR MACRO §D-2 ② 는 *"`기준금리` 가 1.00 → 0.99× 로 처음 꺾였다(3런 상승 후)"* 를 `M-92` 약화 근거로 적었다.
**오늘 1.01× 로 되돌아왔다**(1,959 → 1,969건). ⇒ **하루짜리 배율 변동을 서사 판정으로 읽지 않는다.**
**`M-92` 는 조정 없이 원래 상태로 운반한다.**

## §6 열린 모순 — **`C15` 가 KR 쪽에서 한 칸 더 복잡해진다** (해결하지 않고 운반)

`C15`(= `vol_surge` 게이트: KR 원장은 부호 음, `sector_flow` 🟢 게이트는 양 가중)에 대해,
어제 KR 런은 *"`W1` 은 이 데스크를 막지 않는다 — 원장이 `market=kr` 이고 결정 소유자가 KR"* 이라 적었다.
**`R98`(08-24 US)이 그 절반을 죽였다**: **US 원장 220행에서 같은 축 h=1 이 `t +3.40`(양)**, KR 은 `−3.31`(음),
**둘 다 Bonferroni 통과.**
⇒ **오늘의 상태**: **KR 결정의 소유자는 여전히 KR이다. 그러나 `sector_flow` 는 공유 코드이므로
「고쳐라」가 아니라 「시장별로 갈라라」가 사람에게 갈 질문이다.**
🚫 **이 런도 뒤집지 않는다**(P5). ⚠ **오늘 KR 원장 재적립 후 값**: `vol_surge` h=1 **t −3.31 (n=35, n_eff 35.0)** ·
h=5 **−3.35 (n_eff 5.6)** — **관측이 늘어도 죽지 않았다.**
★ **오늘 이 모순이 만든 실제 비용이 처음 이름으로 측정됐다**: 제약 🟢 7종 **전부**가 `vol_surge ≥ 1.2` 로 켜졌고
**그중 1조+ 는 한미약품 하나**, 나머지 6종 합계 시총 1.66조 = 섹터의 1.2%. 그리고 건설 🟢 1종은 **시총 400억**이다.

⚠⚠ **이 런의 정직한 약점**: **(i)** DEEP 두 섹터가 **에이전트 팬아웃 없이 인컨텍스트·순차** 실행됐다(세션 제약, 선언함) —
두 파일이 이 런의 blind spot 을 공유하고, **각 파일이 「독립 검증 못 한 것」을 목록으로 적었다**(INDU 5개 · HLTH 4개);
**(ii)** **HLTH 시총의 78%(삼성바이오로직스 CDMO + 셀트리온 바이오시밀러)를 손도 대지 않고** 「섹터가 강하다」를 논했다;
**(iii)** **000720 풍문 해명공시가 08-11 것이 14일째 미해소인 채 08-24 에 하나 더 나왔는데 본문을 못 읽었다** —
뉴스 경로가 **동명 여자배구단으로 오염**돼 있고(`"현대건설"` 5일 23건의 상위가 배구 기사) DART 뷰어는 안 열었다 ⇒ `unknown`(C3);
**(iv)** **375500·003230 밸류 다리를 4런 연속 미이행 ⇒ 오늘 「소멸」로 기록하고 목록에서 내렸다**;
**(v)** **자기반증 3건**(위 자기수정), 그중 하나는 **이 런이 회수된 단어를 다시 쓴 것**이다;
**(vi)** 블라인드 커버리지 **11.2%**(702/6,243) — 매크로 텀으로는 국내 뉴스의 88.8%를 못 본다;
**(vii)** 사건축에서 **1매체 161 · 비시장 16 · 몸통 34 = 최소 211건 미열람**.

   side only), the clip is non-linear so the correction is not a shift, and axis-missing names drop out.

   a diagnosis retracted late in run N is **invisible** to the DIFF column in run N+1.

| **`R108`** | ★★★ **"The `rs20` bench-misalignment bias is a constant offset, therefore CROSS-SECTIONAL RANK survives and may be cited."** Written into PREFLIGHT's G0 exemption and used by ROTATION to cite sector ranks and flipper lists for roughly ten runs | **A controlled rebuild of the SAME session refuted it.** Holding session (08-27), universe (806), axes (3) and formulas constant and moving only the bench bar (08-26 → 08-27): **19 of 28 sectors change rank** (유통 **+5**, 미분류 +6, 전기·가스 +4, 비금속 / 운송장비·부품 / 증권 **−3**), universe `wflow` **+0.333 → +0.172**, greens **67 → 44**, and the **flipper set changes membership 5 → 3** (`M1029`). **The mechanism is in the scoring code**: `flow_score` applies `clip(rs20 / 8.0)`, so a constant offset **in pp** is *not* constant **in score space** — names already at the ±1 cap lose nothing, names inside the band lose the full amount. ⇒ **A constant bias in the input is a non-uniform bias in the output whenever the transform saturates.** ★ **And the cost was not only numerical**: 운송장비·부품 was a *flipper* in the misaligned file, so the flipper rule **blocked a demotion that the aligned data permits** — the defect also fired the wrong guard on the wrong bucket | `industry_kr` PREFLIGHT (G0 exemption), carried by ROTATION | 2026-08-29 |
| **`R109`** | **"`module_KIS --investor` returns 10 rows regardless of the argument, therefore EVERY KIS number in a run is labelled 9 settled sessions."** (`M959` / `D370-KR`, reproduced and restated for two consecutive runs) | **The consequential half is false.** Same name, four calls: `--investor 5` returns **5 rows** and a cumulative foreign figure of **−62.1만**; `--investor 20` returns 10 rows and **−149.6만**; `--investor 30` returns 10 rows and **−132.2만** (`M1028`). ## §6 · OPEN CONTRADICTIONS — **`C22` NEWLY REGISTERED** (carried, NOT resolved)

**`C22` — the desk's two ledgers name different hands, and on 2026-08-29 they returned opposite verdicts on the same name in the same run.**
- `kr_live_shortlist` tags **real-hands** on `foreign OR institution` net-buy.
- `reject_ledger` revival conditions have been written as `foreign AND retail-negative`.
- **Measured on `066970` 엘앤에프**: institutions **+164.5만**, foreign **−149.6만**, retail −16.4만, price **109,700 → 134,800 (+22.9%)** over the visible 10 sessions. The shortlist called it **real-hands**; the ledger `reaffirmed` the rejection because foreign was negative. **Both followed their own rule correctly.**
- **Why this is a contradiction and not a bug**: the desk's own inherited board shape is *"13 of 15 candidates are institution-only"*. A revival condition keyed on **foreign** can therefore **never fire on an institution-led move**, which is the modal KR move on this board.
- **A second instance the same run**: the missed-ledger row for `HII` requires *"HII enters the universe via `build_us_universe.py --include`"* — a condition that observes **our own builder**, not the market, and so cannot fire on its own. **2 of the 4 rows due today carried this defect** (against 10 of 10 clean on 08-28, so it is not monotone).
- **Carried, not resolved.** Editing a revival condition at scoring time is `C5`. **Which hand a KR revival condition should name is a human call.**

`C21` (US 08-27, RSI convention) inherited and **carried untouched** — this desk did not run `us_setup_screener`.
`C17` reproduced **in a new layer**: the semiconductor-tariff event is one object that the desk's bucket vocabulary splits across **two buckets** (`반도체` and `관세`), the same failure the sector labels produce for value chains. `C15`·`C16`·`C18`·`C19`·`C14`·`C10`·`C1` carried unchanged. **`C19` gained the `HII` instance above.**

   and the weekday legs go **13 → 21, RISING**. ⇒ **only the term sweep says the narrative is

   the error is recorded rather than deleted and is the direct cause of **`D508`**.

   concluded *"every available straddle EXPIRES BEFORE both binaries… no threshold could be taken
   from the options market."* **True of the three ETFs measured (`SPY`, `SMH`, `XLE`) and not true in

   from `D93` dispersion and stay there. The sentence stands in `BLINDSPOT_PREMORTEM §0b` with its

|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`R130`** | *"「금속이 팔렸다」는 오늘 성립하지 않는다. **「비철이 팔렸다」이다.**"* — **이 런 자신의 SWEEP §2-a**, 노드 분해(`eqflow` 철강 +0.071 vs 비철 −0.395, 스프레드가 섹터 값의 6.2배)를 근거로 | **DEEP-MATR 의 잔차상관, 세 스테이지 뒤.** 벤치(`^KS11`) 베타 제거 후 84거래일: **철강(27)↔비철(5) 노드 동일가중 잔차상관 +0.7153** · **철강×비철 교차 쌍상관 중앙 +0.3905** — 그리고 그 값이 **철강 그룹내 중앙 +0.3834 보다 크다.** 「두 노드」가 서로에게, 각자 자기 안쪽에게보다 더 닮았다. **`risk_units` 임계 0.35 위** ⇒ **분리 불성립.** ★ **살아남는 절반**: `eqflow` **레벨** 차이(+0.045 vs −0.171)는 실재한다 — 회수되는 것은 **그 레벨 차이를 「두 개의 판정 단위」로 읽은 것**이다. **`MATR N` 유지가 옳고, ROTATION 이 승격을 기각한 것도 옳다** | `industry_kr` SWEEP §2-a → DEEP-MATR §0 | 2026-09-06 |

### ⚠ One carried claim DATED rather than retracted

- **`M1323`** (*"the F1 zero is arithmetic — a theme old enough to have a name is structurally
  incapable of being FRESH"*, the **age**-leg explanation) is **dated, not wrong.** This run measured
  the **acceleration** leg passing for the first time in 13 foreign measurements — `Venezuela`
  **2.95×** against a prior maximum of **1.95×** — while the theme still scores **age ≥90** because
  the *word* is old and the **deal driving it is eight days old**. ⇒ **`D543`**: `theme-age`'s age
  leg measures the age of the **word**, not the age of the **event**, so the gate cannot mark a new
  event on an old proper noun as fresh. **A better explanation of 13 zeros than "no fresh themes
  exist," and a fixable defect rather than a market fact.**

# ═══ §5 APPEND — 회수 원장 · `industry_kr` 2026-09-07 (월) ═══

> **append-only.** 원문을 고치지 않는다. 무너진 문장을 인용하고, 무너뜨린 측정을 옆에 둔다.

| id | 회수된 주장 (원문 인용) | 무너뜨린 측정 | 어디서 | 날짜 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`R136`** | *"처방은 「더 많은 텀」이 아니라 **「더 긴 텀」**이다"* — **어제 이 데스크가 `M-132` (a) 갈래에 박은 처방 조항**(2글자 머리단어 축은 4글자 동의어로 갈아라) | **오늘 `search`(LIKE) 경로 실측.** 4글자 대체어 `외환시장` 7일 **198건** 은 사건축 FX 스레드 **320건**의 **61.9%** 만 잡는데, **원래 2글자 표면형 `환율` 이 `search` 경로에서 창이 3.5배 짧은데도(2일) 119건**을 돌려준다(`M1385`·`M1386`). ⇒ **처방은 「더 긴 텀」이 아니라 「다른 계기」다.** ★ **살아남는 절반, 그리고 오히려 강해졌다**: **진단(2글자는 `fts` trigram 색인에 없다)** 은 창을 3일→7일로 늘려도 `환율`·`금리`·`관세`·`파병` **전부 0** 으로 재현됐다. **회수되는 것은 처방이지 진단이 아니다** | `industry_kr` MACRO §D → §C-1/C-2 | 2026-09-07 |
| **`R137`** | *"보험 4종이 한 위험단위이므로 **보험 섹터가 한 단위**"* — **이 데스크가 `M1272` 를 인용할 때마다 해 온 축약**(어제 ROTATION·DEEP 두 곳) | **오늘 DEEP-FIN 의 잔차상관 행렬**(84거래일, 벤치 `^KS11`, 임계 0.35): **🟢 4종 +0.6806**[0.634~0.816] 은 재현되는데 **보험 12종으로 넓히면 +0.3556**[−0.031~+0.816] 이고 **생명 4종은 +0.1850 으로 단위가 아니다**(`M1396`). ★ **살아남는 절반**: **`M1272` 자체는 4종에 대해 참이고 오늘 재현됐다** — 회수되는 것은 **부분의 응집도를 전체로 확대한 축약**이다. ⇒ **breadth 0.33 = 「1단위의 4중 계수」는 오히려 확정됐다** | `industry_kr` DEEP-FIN §1 | 2026-09-07 |
| **`R138`** | *"「보험 `OW−`」와 「금융 `N−`」는 두 개의 판정이다"* — **이 데스크가 09-05 승격 이후 3런 동안 유지한 구분** | **같은 행렬의 교차 다리.** **보험×은행 교차 중앙 +0.4210 (쌍 60) > 보험 그룹내 +0.3556**(`M1397`) ⇒ **보험은 자기들끼리보다 은행과 더 닮았다.** ★ **어제 `R130` 이 금속에서 찾은 것과 정확히 같은 형태**(*"두 노드가 서로에게, 각자 자기 안쪽에게보다 더 닮았다 ⇒ 분리 불성립"*). ⇒ **두 판정을 따로 굴리면 같은 베팅을 두 번 세는 것**이고, **저축은행 충당금 역풍은 보험 OW 에도 걸린다.** ★ **살아남는 절반**: **판정 자체(`FIN N−`·보험 `OW−`)는 오늘 바꾸지 않았다** — 새 흐름 관측이 0이므로 바뀐 것은 **단위와 근거**이지 방향이 아니다 | `industry_kr` DEEP-FIN §1-b → §6 | 2026-09-07 |

### ⚠ 이 런이 **자기 안에서** 잡은 반증 — **4건, 그리고 세 건이 같은 클래스다**

1. ★★★ **HANDOVER 의 KR 과거일 back-scan 이 두 번 연속 거짓 ✅ 를 냈다.**
   ① id 주변 ±300자 검색 ⇒ **23/23 ✅**(옆 행의 판정을 매칭) · ② id 와 판정 토큰의 **같은 줄** 동시등장 ⇒ **역시 23/23 ✅**
   (*"Condition-check executed on every ARMED row…"* 같은 **요약 행**이 여러 id 를 나열하며 판정 토큰도 담는다; `S11`·`S18`·`S47-KR`
   의 히트는 실제로는 **`"NOT EXPIRED"`·`"PENDING"`** 이었다 — **부정문이 긍정 판정으로 읽혔다**).
   **테이블 첫 칸이 그 id 인 행만** 보는 세 번째 시도에서야 신호가 깨끗해졌다.
   ⇒ **어제 US 가 `D531` 로 등록한 실패 기제가 오늘 내 도구 안에서 재현됐다** — 그것은 US 파일의 특성이 아니라 **verdict-grep 자체의 특성**이다.
2. ★★ **BET 이 「원장이 0 due 를 보고하는데 재확인일(09-05) 지난 `377300` 이 미해소로 앉아 있다」는 결함 가설을 세웠고, 한 명령이 반박했다.**
   `out/reject_ledger.jsonl` 303행에는 `outcome` 필드가 **0건**이지만 **해소는 `out/reject_ledger_resolutions.jsonl` 에 별도 저장**되고
   **`377300` 은 거기 `reaffirmed`(3다리 중 1개 미충족)로 있다.** `due` 의 소스도 확인했다 — `resolved_keys` 로 정확히 배제한다.
   ⇒ **원장은 건강하고 결함은 내 스캔에 있었다.**
3. ★ **DEEP-IT 이 「메모리가 `IT OW−` 의 근거」라는 상속된 전제를 세웠다가 같은 표가 무너뜨렸다** — 노드 분해 결과 **메모리는 7노드 중 3위(+0.263),
   `rs60` 음(−4.0)** 이고 **최상위는 배터리·소재(+0.605)** 다(`M1392`). **09-01·09-03·09-05·09-06 네 런의 DEEP-IT 이 이 분해를 하지 않았다.**
4. ★ **DEEP-FIN 이 「보험 4종 = 1단위 ⇒ 보험 섹터 = 1단위」로 출발했다가 측정이 무너뜨렸다**(⇒ `R137`).

🚨 **①②는 같은 뿌리다: 두-파일 시스템을 한 파일만 읽고 결론냈다.** ①은 `SCENARIOS_KR.md` 만 보고 마스터 로그의 행 구조를 무시했고,
②는 `reject_ledger.jsonl` 만 보고 `_resolutions.jsonl` 을 안 열었다. ⇒ **`D549` 로 등록한다.**

### ⚠ Claims this run asserted and then refuted **inside the same run** — written down, not edited away (`D48`)

1. ★★★ **A full share-normalised term table was computed, formatted and then deleted** — median
   **+11.9%**, 16 of 18 terms up more than +5%. It would have read as *"attention flooded into
   everything"*, the mirror image of the 09-06 run's *"attention drained from everything"*, and
   **neither is a thing that happens.** The control test killed it before publication. The sentence
   is left standing in `MACRO_REPORT §F-4` rather than removed.
2. ⚠ **A first attempt normalised yesterday's counts against a denominator re-pulled today**
   (09-06 = 1,521 rather than the 557 the 09-06 run saw) — a **maturity mismatch** that inflated
   every term. Caught before the second draft. Recorded as a distinct error from (1) because it
   would recur independently (`D558`).
3. ★★ **The news wall was first read as "gone — scattered with gaps everywhere"**, then refuted by
   the next command (⇒ `R140`).
4. ⚠ **This run made direct news calls and did not body-read them at the term-sweep stage** — the
   same habit `R134`/`D541` named that morning. It **did** body-read at EVENT_ALPHA and DEEP (six
   filings and articles opened in full), so the instance is partial rather than clean. **Recorded as
   an instance, not excused.**

Measured: FX thread 09-06+09-07 = **137** vs `search 환율 --days 2` = **226** = **+65%**. The two instruments do not share a unit, so a ±30% band between them was never a testable condition. ⇒ **The leg is withdrawn; `M-135` is scored on leg ① alone (all four terms >0 on `search`, 0 on `fts`), run 1 of 3.** The diagnosis is untouched | 2026-09-08 |
| **R146** | **"The `fts` two-character failure is worked around by quoted juxtaposition — `\"A\" \"B\"`"** — carried in the operator's own notes and used as the escape hatch for the KR trigram defect | **Measured false for 2-char tokens, this run.** `"원화" "강세"` = **0** · `"달러" "약세"` = **0** · `"환율" "최저"` = **0**, while the same day's FX thread ran **362 articles over 7 days, the board's largest**. ⇒ **The failure sits at the TOKEN, not at the query syntax.** The workaround survives **only for tokens of 3+ characters** and is narrowed to that. **Consequence, stated plainly: the ② FX bucket cannot see the week's dominant macro event by any `fts` form** — `원화강세` 5 · `달러약세` 0 · `원화절상` 0 | 2026-09-08 |

# ═══ §5 APPEND — retracted ledger · `industry_US` 2026-09-08 (Tue) ═══

> Append-only, shared spine. This run produced **one** retraction and it kills a **cause**, not an

| id | retracted claim | what killed it | source | date |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`R147`** | **`D427`'s stated cause — *"H.15 does not publish on a federal holiday, so `DGS2`/`DGS10`/`DFII10` are blocked"*** — carried through **nine reproductions**, and used by the 09-06 and 09-07 runs to **pre-commit** that the block would lift on the first post-holiday business day (`M1412` logged that pre-commitment as a success) | **The dates refute the mechanism.** The block was **already present on Saturday 2026-09-05** — the 09-05 run's own §A prints `DGS2` at 09-03 and `T10YIE` at 09-04 — a day whose only intervening event was Friday's close. **A federal holiday two days later cannot explain a block that pre-dates it**, and the block then **survived the first post-holiday business morning** (this run, 6th consecutive). ★ And the split was proven **FRED-side, not module-side**, by bypassing `module_macro_us` entirely and querying the REST API for raw ids: `DGS10`/`DGS2`/`DFII10`/`VIXCLS`/`BAMLH0A0HYM2` all end **2026-09-03**, `T10YIE` ends **2026-09-04** (`M1455`) | `MACRO_REPORT` 2026-09-08 §A-1 | 2026-09-08 |

### ★ What survives, and what the desk must stop doing
**Survives:** the **observation** — nine reproductions of a split H.15 publication, and `P121`/`P114`/
`P125` blocked for a **6th** consecutive run.
**Withdrawn:** the **mechanism**, and with it the desk's licence to predict the unblock date. ⇒ **stop
forecasting when these three series clear.** `SECTOR_DEEP_FIN` acted on this in the same run by
registering **`P148`** on **ETFs** (`IEF` − `SHY`) precisely so `D427` cannot block a live bracket.

### ⚠ Claims this run asserted and then refuted **inside the same run** (`D48`) — 5, written down not edited away

1. ★★ **The term sweep's first pass ran all 18 terms in the WRONG CONVENTION** and would have been
   published as the phrase column. Against the 09-07 phrase numbers it produced **`credit spread`
   +466%** and **`AI capex` +316%** — a fabricated *"attention flooded into credit and AI capex"*
   story. Caught by a two-term control (`rate hike` unquoted **1,382** vs quoted **1,746**) before it
   was written. `D473`/`C5` reproducing **inside** the stage that cites them.
2. ★★ **The first read of the `S13` capex leg took an article HEADLINE — *"Meta Stock Falls Sharply on
   Weak Earnings and CapEx Boost"* — as evidence capex was RAISED.** The **body of the same article**
   refuted it: *"narrowed its expectations for 2026 capital expenditures."* Had the headline been
   trusted, `S13` would have scored **`FIRED-B`** instead of `AMBIGUOUS`. **`R134`/`D541`'s first
   measured US save.**

# §5 addendum — retraction filed by the 2026-09-09 `industry_kr` run

| id | claim retracted | what killed it | source | date |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`R148`** | **"The news-API cooldown after a sweep is 65–85 seconds"** — promoted to a CONFIRMED constant by the 2026-09-07 run, which closed both bounds in one run (+5/20/40/60s fail, +80s recover) and recorded it as settled | **Disjoint on re-measurement.** Same procedure, 2026-09-09: sweep ended 08:22:13; probes at **+42s, +70s, +86s, +102s all FAILED** and **+118s recovered** ⇒ **102–118s**, which does not overlap 65–85s. ★ And the causal label is refuted too: the sweep's **last news call was ~08:18:4x** (velocity covers universe positions 0–52, scored early), so a probe **4 minutes after the last news request still failed**, while recovery tracked **118s after the sweep PROCESS ended**. The error string is `URLError(FileNotFoundError)` — a **local OS error, not HTTP 429/401** — and a **separate process inherited the failure**, so the state is machine-wide, not per-process. ⇒ **"Cooldown = 65–85s" is withdrawn; "the news server banned us" is withdrawn as a label.** ✅ What survives: **the pipe is unusable for roughly 1–2 minutes after a sweep, it is not a constant (n=2, disjoint), and the cause is undecomposed (`C3`).** Working hypothesis for the next run: ephemeral-port/socket exhaustion from the 833-symbol batch download (TCP `TIME_WAIT` ≈120s). **Discriminating test prepared: `netstat -an \| grep -c TIME_WAIT` measured 58 post-recovery (262 TCP total) — the next run measures it immediately after the sweep.** | `preflight/PREFLIGHT.md` 2026-09-09 §G1-a | 2026-09-09 |
